#+begin_src javascript
import { Freight, Path } from "@vivalence/typology";
import paladin from "@vivalence/paladin";
import { mountMode } from "@vivalence/runtime/scenarios";
import { entities } from "../domain/voffice/entities/index.js";

export const office = async (viva, { mountpoint = null, statics = viva.statics, cortex = null } = {}) => {
  const scenario = await mountMode(viva, { entities, ...(cortex && { cortex }) });
  const { manifest } = viva;
  scenario.daemon.datamap = scenario.datamap;
  scenario.mode.entity.installed = "";
  scenario.mode.module.mount = new Path(
    new URL(`../modes/${manifest.type}/${manifest.slug}/${manifest.slug}.viva.js`, import.meta.url).pathname,
  );
  scenario.mode.statics = statics;
  if (mountpoint) {
    scenario.mode.mountpoint = new Path(mountpoint);
    const files = await paladin.find.walk(/./, statics.ignore)(mountpoint);
    scenario.mode.freight = new Freight(mountpoint).stow(files.map((file) => file.absolute.slice(mountpoint.length + 1)));
  }
  return scenario;
};

export const jdex = async () => {
  const root = await Deno.makeTempDir();
  const put = async (path, content) => {
    await Deno.mkdir(`${root}/${path.split("/").slice(0, -1).join("/")}`, { recursive: true });
    if (content instanceof Uint8Array) await Deno.writeFile(`${root}/${path}`, content);
    else await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/${path}`, content);
  };
  await put("20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Rechnungen/RE24-05277.pdf", new Uint8Array([0x25, 0x50, 0x44, 0x46]));
  await put("20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.00 Finance index.md", "---\ntitle: Finance\n---\n\n# Finance\n");
  await put("10-19_viva/12_goals/12.04 Ubiquity and a Thin Slice.md", "# Ubiquity and a Thin Slice\n\ntext\n");
  await put("10-19_viva/12_goals/notes.md", "loose notes\n");
  await put("readme.org", "#+TITLE: The company\n\n* hello\n");
  await put("jdex-sbs/00-09 System/00.00 JDex.md", "template\n");
  await put("bak/old.md", "stale\n");
  return root;
};
#+end_src

import { specimen } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { coordinates, slugOf, coordinate } from "../jd.js";

const slug = (path, seen = new Set()) => slugOf(coordinates(path), { path }, seen);

specimen.describe("jd — coordinates", () => {
  specimen.it("directories mint area, category and id; a file mints only its own id", () => {
    const { symbols, prefix, name } = coordinates("20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Rechnungen/RE24-05277.pdf");
    specimen.expect(symbols.map((symbol) => symbol.slug)).toEqual(["vdex.area.20-29", "vdex.category.22", "vdex.id.22.04"]);
    specimen.expect(symbols[0].trait.LABELED).toEqual({ name: "koerperschaft", description: "20-29_koerperschaft" });
    specimen.expect(prefix).toBe("22.04");
    specimen.expect(name).toBeNull();
    specimen.expect(coordinates("12 notes.md").symbols).toEqual([]);
  });

  specimen.it("a NN.00 file carries the index slot and its own name", () => {
    const { symbols, name } = coordinates("30-39_architecture/30.00_architecture.mdx");
    specimen.expect(symbols.map((symbol) => symbol.slug)).toEqual(["vdex.area.30-39", "vdex.id.30.00", "vdex.slot.index"]);
    specimen.expect(name).toBe("architecture");
  });

  specimen.it("both grammars — underscore and space", () => {
    specimen.expect(coordinates("10-19 Business administration/11 The business/11.10 Official documents/x.pdf").symbols.map((symbol) => symbol.slug))
      .toEqual(["vdex.area.10-19", "vdex.category.11", "vdex.id.11.10"]);
  });
});

specimen.describe("jd — slugs", () => {
  specimen.it("deepest coordinate · folded stem · extension", () => {
    specimen.expect(slug("20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Rechnungen/Notariat/RE24-05277.pdf")).toBe("22.04.re24-05277.pdf");
    specimen.expect(slug("10-19_viva/12_goals/12.04 Ubiquity and a Thin Slice.md")).toBe("12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md");
    specimen.expect(slug("30-39_architecture/30.00_architecture.mdx")).toBe("30.00.architecture.mdx");
    specimen.expect(slug("10-19_viva/12_goals/notes.md")).toBe("12.notes.md");
    specimen.expect(slug("00.00 Index.org")).toBe("00.00.index.org");
    specimen.expect(slug("readme.org")).toBe("readme.org");
    specimen.expect(slug("10-19_viva/18_vinca/Ünïcode Straße.pdf")).toBe("18.unicode-stra-e.pdf");
  });

  specimen.it("a collision under one coordinate gets -2 before the extension", () => {
    const seen = new Set();
    specimen.expect(slug("22.04_accounting/a/x.md", seen)).toBe("22.04.x.md");
    specimen.expect(slug("22.04_accounting/b/x.md", seen)).toBe("22.04.x-2.md");
  });

  specimen.it("coordinate() reads the three id shapes", () => {
    specimen.expect(coordinate("22")).toBe("vdex.category.22");
    specimen.expect(coordinate("22.04")).toBe("vdex.id.22.04");
    specimen.expect(coordinate("20-29")).toBe("vdex.area.20-29");
  });
});

#+begin_src javascript
import { specimen } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as runtime from "@vivalence/runtime";
import { office, jdex } from "../../../../tests/scenarios.js";
import * as vdex from "../vdex.viva.js";
import { boot, reindex } from "../boot.js";
import { index } from "../index.js";

specimen.describe("vdex boot", () => {
  let scenario, root;

  specimen.beforeAll(async () => {
    root = await jdex();
    scenario = await office(vdex, { mountpoint: root });
    await scenario.scoped(() => runtime.daemon.traits.DATASET(scenario.mode, scenario.daemon));
    await scenario.scoped(() => boot(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode));
  });

  specimen.afterAll(async () => {
    await scenario?.orm?.close();
    await Deno.remove(root, { recursive: true });
  });

  specimen.it("indexes every document the ignore list lets through", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const rows = await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.find({ ontology: "vdex" });
      specimen.expect(rows.map((row) => row.slug).sort()).toEqual([
        "12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md",
        "12.notes.md",
        "22.00.finance-index.md",
        "22.04.re24-05277.pdf",
        "readme.org",
      ]);
      const pdf = rows.find((row) => row.slug === "22.04.re24-05277.pdf");
      specimen.expect(pdf.trait.DOCUMENT.readable).toBe(true);
      specimen.expect(pdf.symbol).toEqual({ vdex: { area: "20-29", category: "22", id: { "22": "04" }, format: "pdf" } });
      specimen.expect(pdf.ontology).toBe("vdex");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("mints the coordinates and lets the NN.00 index name its category", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const symbols = await scenario.daemon.entities.symbol.find({ slug: { $like: "vdex.%" } });
      const slugs = symbols.map((symbol) => symbol.slug);
      for (const slug of ["vdex.area.20-29", "vdex.category.22", "vdex.id.22.04", "vdex.area.10-19", "vdex.category.12", "vdex.id.12.04", "vdex.id.22.00"])
        specimen.expect(slugs).toContain(slug);
      specimen.expect(symbols.find((symbol) => symbol.slug === "vdex.category.22").trait.LABELED.name).toBe("Finance");
      specimen.expect(symbols.find((symbol) => symbol.slug === "vdex.area.20-29").trait.LABELED.name).toBe("koerperschaft");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("renders the index as the harness section", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const text = await index(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode);
      specimen.expect(text.split("\n")[0]).toBe(`[VDex — ${root}]`);
      specimen.expect(text).toContain("  22 Finance");
      specimen.expect(text).toContain("    22.04 accounting · 1 · pdf");
      specimen.expect(text).toContain("unfiled · 1");
      const tight = await index(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode, { budget: 80 });
      specimen.expect(tight).not.toContain("22.04");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("a reindex after a delete prunes the row and the orphaned coordinate", async () => {
    await Deno.remove(`${root}/10-19_viva/12_goals/12.04 Ubiquity and a Thin Slice.md`);
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      specimen.expect(await reindex(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode)).toEqual({ literal: 1, symbol: 1 });
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md" })).toBeNull();
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug: "vdex.id.12.04" })).toBeNull();
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug: "vdex" })).not.toBeNull();
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug: "vdex.slot.index" })).not.toBeNull();
    });
  });

  specimen.it("a reindex after an own write keeps the slug and moves the hash", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const before = await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.notes.md" });
      await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/10-19_viva/12_goals/notes.md`, "loose notes, edited\n");
      await reindex(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode);
      const after = await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.notes.md" }, { refresh: true });
      specimen.expect(after.trait.DOCUMENT.hash).not.toBe(before.trait.DOCUMENT.hash);
    });
  });

  specimen.it("a file that appears on disk lands as a row and in the cargo", async () => {
    await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/10-19_viva/12_goals/12.07 Roadmap.md`, "# Roadmap\n");
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      await reindex(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode);
      specimen.expect((await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.07.roadmap.md" })).trait.DOCUMENT.title).toBe("Roadmap");
      specimen.expect(scenario.mode.freight.catalog["10-19_viva/12_goals/12.07 Roadmap.md"]).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});
#+end_src

import { specimen, shape, Url } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as runtime from "@vivalence/runtime";
import { office, jdex } from "../../../../tests/scenarios.js";
import * as vdex from "../vdex.viva.js";
import { boot } from "../boot.js";

specimen.describe("vdex tools", () => {
  let scenario, root, tools;

  specimen.beforeAll(async () => {
    root = await jdex();
    scenario = await office(vdex, { mountpoint: root });
    scenario.mode.freight.withUrl(new Url("http://test/attached/cargo/daemon/test-daemon"));
    await scenario.scoped(() => runtime.daemon.traits.DATASET(scenario.mode, scenario.daemon));
    await scenario.scoped(() => boot(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode));
    tools = shape.object(scenario.mode.tools);
  });

  specimen.afterAll(async () => {
    await scenario?.orm?.close();
    await Deno.remove(root, { recursive: true });
  });

  specimen.it("find resolves a category, an id, an area, and text", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ id: "22" })).output.literal.map((row) => row.slug).sort()).toEqual(["22.00.finance-index.md", "22.04.re24-05277.pdf"]);
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ id: "22.04" })).output.literal.map((row) => row.slug)).toEqual(["22.04.re24-05277.pdf"]);
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ id: "10-19" })).output.literal.length).toBe(2);
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ text: "ubiquity" })).output.message).toContain("12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md");
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ text: "nothing-here" })).output.message).toBe("nothing filed there.");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("fs verbs speak from the mountpoint; a pdf read answers binary with its cargo url", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const tree = await tools.fs.tree({});
      specimen.expect(tree.output.message).toContain("20-29_koerperschaft/");
      specimen.expect(tree.output.message).not.toContain("jdex-sbs");
      const text = await tools.fs.read({ path: "readme.org" });
      specimen.expect(text.output.message).toContain("#+TITLE: The company");
      const binary = await tools.fs.read({ path: "20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Rechnungen/RE24-05277.pdf" });
      specimen.expect(binary.output.message).toContain("binary");
      specimen.expect(binary.output.message).toContain("/attached/cargo/daemon/test-daemon/20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Rechnungen/RE24-05277.pdf");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("fs_write reindexes the file it wrote", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      await tools.fs.write({ path: "30-39_funding/30_applications/30.01_content/pitch.md", content: "# Pitch\n\nhello\n" });
      const row = await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "30.01.pitch.md" });
      specimen.expect(row.trait.DOCUMENT.title).toBe("Pitch");
      specimen.expect(row.symbol.vdex.id).toEqual({ "30": "01" });
    });
  });

  specimen.it("open puts the document on screen as a buffer", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const opened = await tools.open({ slug: "readme.org" });
      specimen.expect(opened.output.buffer[0].data.open).toBe("readme.org");
      const missing = await tools.open({ slug: "nope.md" });
      specimen.expect(missing.condition).toBe("ERROR");
    });
  });
});

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/tests/vdex.snapshot.test.js
new — /"snapshots up the wazoo"/. The house =specimen.snapshot= CAPTURES (writes the json every
run; drift is read in the diff — =mode-entity.snapshot.test.js=, =oracle.snapshot.test.js=), it
does not assert; the asserts beside it pin the invariants. One scenario, one mock dialogue
faculty that KEEPS the assembled Request (=via.render= receives it) — that is the prompt
snapshot. Five captures into =tests/snapshots/=: the index text · the hallucination record
(system sections + the armed catalog with valences + policy) · the rows (literals + symbols) ·
every tool call · every aperture route + the aperture contract. =stable()= drops ids and
timestamps, pins =mtime= and the temp root; hashes stay (the fixture content is fixed). The
same five land per office mode (email · chat) and per officer (their prompts) — the doctrine
for the package: a mode ships its snapshots.

#+begin_src javascript
import { specimen, shape, Url, Cortex } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as runtime from "@vivalence/runtime";
import { office, jdex } from "../../../../tests/scenarios.js";
import * as vdex from "../vdex.viva.js";
import { boot } from "../boot.js";
import { index } from "../index.js";

const { snapshot } = specimen;
const base = new URL("./snapshots", import.meta.url).pathname;
const DRY = false;

let captured = null;
const seer = () => ({
  type: "dialogue",
  tune: [0.9, 1.0, 0.3],
  context: 200000,
  channels: { in: ["text", "tool_result"], out: ["text", "tool_use"] },
  via: {
    render: async (request) => {
      captured = request;
      return { role: "assistant", parts: [{ type: "text", text: "noted." }], meta: { state: "complete" } };
    },
  },
});

const catalog = (vector, prefix = "") =>
  [...vector.trie.values()].flatMap(({ pattern, trajectory }) => {
    const path = `${prefix}/${pattern.nature}`;
    const leaf = trajectory.effect ? [{ path, valence: pattern.valence ?? null }] : [];
    return [...leaf, ...catalog(trajectory, path)];
  });

let scenario, root, tools;

const stable = (value) =>
  JSON.parse(
    JSON.stringify(value, (key, held) =>
      ["id", "createdAt", "updatedAt", "url"].includes(key) ? undefined : key === "mtime" ? "<mtime>" : held,
    ).replaceAll(root, "<mount>"),
  );
const rows = async (repo, where) => (await repo.find(where, { orderBy: { slug: "asc" } })).map((row) => row.toJSON());
const capture = (subject, locate) => snapshot(subject, { base, dry: DRY, locate, parse: stable });

specimen.beforeAll(async () => {
  root = await jdex();
  scenario = await office(vdex, { mountpoint: root, cortex: new Cortex().register([seer()]) });
  scenario.mode.freight.withUrl(new Url("http://test/attached/cargo/daemon/test-daemon"));
  await scenario.scoped(() => runtime.daemon.traits.DATASET(scenario.mode, scenario.daemon));
  await scenario.scoped(() => boot(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode));

});

  await Deno.remove(root, { recursive: true });
});

specimen.describe("vdex snapshots", () => {
  specimen.it("the index — the harness section as text", async () => {

    });

    await scenario.scoped(async () => {

      const spoken = {

        find_id: await tools.find({ id: "22.04" }),
        find_area: await tools.find({ id: "10-19" }),
        find_text: await tools.find({ text: "ubiquity" }),
        find_none: await tools.find({ text: "nothing-here" }),
        fs_tree: await tools.fs.tree({}),
        fs_find: await tools.fs.find({ pattern: "\\.md$" }),
        fs_stat: await tools.fs.stat({ path: "readme.org" }),
        fs_read_text: await tools.fs.read({ path: "readme.org" }),
        fs_read_binary: await tools.fs.read({ path: pdf }),
        open: await tools.open({ slug: "readme.org" }),
        open_missing: await tools.open({ slug: "nope.md" }),

      const call = (route, input = {}) => scenario.conn.call(`/mode/office/vdex${route}`, input);
      const before = await call("/document", { slug: "12.notes.md" });
      const written = await call("/write", { slug: "12.notes.md", text: "loose notes, rewritten\n" });

        write: written,
        document_after: await call("/document", { slug: "12.notes.md" }),
        contract: catalog(scenario.mode.aperture).map((leaf) => leaf.path),

*** subsystems/paladin/belt/ignore.js
new (T1.b). The one declaration — a glob list with the =.bak= suffix rule IN it as data, and the
predicate compiled once per walk. The connoisseur's pass on the first cut (his /"i dont like this
yet. something is off"/) — what was off:

- the walker grew a bag: =ignore= threaded as a fourth positional through =search= and an options
  object on the call. The shape of a walk (what to keep, what to prune) belongs in the curried
  head beside =pattern=; the call stays =(path, depth)= and no consumer changes.
- =extra= was an epicycle: two lists unioned (=IGNORE ∪ extra=) plus a suffix rule outside both.
  ONE list, the whole list, defaulted to the declaration; a caller with its own list passes the
  whole list (his =daemon.js= entry already does). Explicit over a hidden union.
- =describe= complected stat with content: every file read and UTF-8-decoded (pdfs too) to fnv1a
  it, and T1.#2 then needed ={hash: false}= to skip the read — the second epicycle. =describe= is
  a stat; the content hash belongs to the reader that already holds the text (the vdex loader).
- string surgery where =@std/path= has the verb: =extname= for the format, =relative= for the path,
  =parse(path).name= for the stem.
- =skills/fs.js= read the house list only — an officer's =vdex_fs_tree= showed =jdex-sbs= while the
  index hid it. The bind that sets =ctx.root= sets =ctx.ignore=; the skill compiles it per call.

MEASURED: =globToRegExp("*.bak")= → =^[^/]*\.bak\/*$= (anchored, one segment); =.git= does not match
=.gitignore=; =extname(".DS_Store")= → ="="; =relative("/r", "/r/a/b.md")= → =a/b.md=.

#+begin_src javascript
import { globToRegExp } from "@std/path";

export const IGNORE = ["bak", "archive", "slp", "node_modules", ".git", ".DS_Store", "*.bak"];

export const skip = (globs = IGNORE) => {
  const rules = globs.map((glob) => globToRegExp(glob));
  return (name) => rules.some((rule) => rule.test(name));
};
#+end_src

*** subsystems/paladin/belt/find.js
edit (T1.b). The walker's head takes the list beside the pattern — =walk(pattern, ignore)= — and
compiles it once; the call =(path, depth)= is unchanged, so =viva= · =json= · =data= · =type= and
every consumer (FRAUGHT, =stamp()=, the ghost, the vip) keep their shape and the house default.
Two verbs beside them: =describe(root, path)= → ={path, format, bytes, mtime}= or =null= (a stat,
nothing read); =index(root, ignore)= → every file under the root, described, sorted by path.
=skip= rides =paladin.find= for a walker outside paladin (the T1.#2 watcher).

#+begin_src javascript
import { assert, assertEquals } from "@std/assert";
import paladin from "@vivalence/paladin";

Deno.test("paladin find — describe · index · the ignore list", async (t) => {
  const root = await Deno.makeTempDir();
  await Deno.mkdir(`${root}/20-29_a/22_b`, { recursive: true });
  await Deno.mkdir(`${root}/bak`);
  await Deno.mkdir(`${root}/old.bak`);
  await Deno.mkdir(`${root}/template`);
  await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/20-29_a/22_b/x.md`, "x");
  await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/readme.org`, "r");
  await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/bak/stale.md`, "s");
  await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/old.bak/stale.md`, "s");
  await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/template/t.md`, "t");
  await Deno.writeTextFile(`${root}/.DS_Store`, "");

  await t.step("describe stats one file, null for what is not a file", async () => {
    const file = await paladin.find.describe(root, "20-29_a/22_b/x.md");
    assertEquals(Object.keys(file), ["path", "format", "bytes", "mtime"]);
    assertEquals(file.path, "20-29_a/22_b/x.md");
    assertEquals(file.format, "md");
    assertEquals(file.bytes, 1);
    assert(file.mtime);
    assertEquals(await paladin.find.describe(root, "20-29_a"), null);
    assertEquals(await paladin.find.describe(root, "missing.md"), null);
  });

  await t.step("index walks the root sorted under the house list", async () => {
    const files = await paladin.find.index(root);
    assertEquals(files.map((file) => file.path), ["20-29_a/22_b/x.md", "readme.org", "template/t.md"]);
  });

  await t.step("a caller's list is the whole list — nothing is unioned in", async () => {
    assertEquals(
      (await paladin.find.index(root, ["template", "bak", "*.bak", ".DS_Store"])).map((file) => file.path),
      ["20-29_a/22_b/x.md", "readme.org"],
    );
    assertEquals(
      (await paladin.find.index(root, ["template"])).map((file) => file.path),
      [".DS_Store", "20-29_a/22_b/x.md", "bak/stale.md", "old.bak/stale.md", "readme.org"],
    );
  });

  await t.step("skip compiles the list once and answers names", () => {
    const skipped = paladin.find.skip(["*.bak", "tmp"]);
    assertEquals([skipped("x.bak"), skipped("tmp"), skipped("bak"), skipped(".bak.md")], [true, true, false, false]);
  });

  await Deno.remove(root, { recursive: true });
});
#+end_src

fingerprint.

specimen.describe("what install does with a loader", () => {
  specimen.it("pulls rows from load(mode), links them, and stamp() folds the loader's stamp", async () => {
    const { DATASET, stamp } = await import("../daemon/traits/index.js");
    let mark = "one";
    const loaded = {
      ...mode,
      slug: "loaded",
      entity: { installed: "" },
      module: {
        mount: mode.module.mount,
        dataset: new Dataset({
          symbol: { load: async () => [{ slug: "jd.id.22.04", traits: ["STRUCTURAL"], trait: {} }], stamp: async () => mark },
          literal: { load: async () => [{ slug: "22.04.re.pdf", ontology: "jd", traits: [], trait: {}, symbols: [{ slug: "jd.id.22.04" }] }] },
        }),
      },
    };
    await DATASET(loaded, daemon);
    const row = await scenario.repos.literal.findOne({ slug: "22.04.re.pdf" }, { populate: ["symbols"] });
    specimen.expect(row.symbols.getItems().map((ref) => ref.slug)).toEqual(["jd.id.22.04"]);
    specimen.expect(row.symbol).toEqual({ jd: { id: { "22": "04" } } });
    const before = await stamp(loaded);
    mark = "two";
    specimen.expect(await stamp(loaded)).not.toBe(before);
  });
});

#+begin_src javascript
const KEYWORD_RE = /^#\+(\w+):\s*(.*)$/;
const HEADING_RE = /^(\*+)\s+(.+)$/;
const SRC_OPEN_RE = /^#\+begin_src\s*(\S*)\s*$/i;
const SRC_CLOSE_RE = /^#\+end_src\s*$/i;
const BLOCK_OPEN_RE = /^#\+begin_(quote|example)\s*$/i;
const BLOCK_CLOSE_RE = /^#\+end_(quote|example)\s*$/i;
const LIST_ITEM_RE = /^(\s*)([-+]|\d+[.)])\s+(.+)$/;
const TABLE_RE = /^\s*\|/;
const TABLE_RULE_RE = /^\s*\|[-+|]+\|?\s*$/;
const INLINE_RE = /(\[\[[^\]]+?\](?:\[[^\]]+?\])?\]|\*[^*\n]+?\*|\/[^/\n]+?\/|~[^~\n]+?~|=[^=\n]+?=|_[^_\n]+?_)/g;

export function parseBlocks(text) {
  if (!text) return [];
  const lines = text.split("\n");
  const blocks = [];
  let i = 0;

  while (i < lines.length) {
    const line = lines[i];

    if (line.trim() === "") {
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }

    const keyword = line.match(KEYWORD_RE);
    if (keyword) {
      if (keyword[1].toUpperCase() === "TITLE") blocks.push({ kind: "heading", level: 1, inline: parseInline(keyword[2]) });
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }

    const src = line.match(SRC_OPEN_RE);
    if (src) {
      const codeLines = [];
      i += 1;
      while (i < lines.length && !SRC_CLOSE_RE.test(lines[i])) codeLines.push(lines[i++]);
      i += 1;
      blocks.push({ kind: "code-block", lang: src[1] || "", text: codeLines.join("\n") });
      continue;
    }

    const block = line.match(BLOCK_OPEN_RE);
    if (block) {
      const inner = [];
      i += 1;
      while (i < lines.length && !BLOCK_CLOSE_RE.test(lines[i])) inner.push(lines[i++]);
      i += 1;
      blocks.push(
        block[1].toLowerCase() === "example"
          ? { kind: "code-block", lang: "", text: inner.join("\n") }
          : { kind: "blockquote", inline: parseInline(inner.join(" ")) },
      );
      continue;
    }

    const heading = line.match(HEADING_RE);
    if (heading) {
      blocks.push({ kind: "heading", level: Math.min(heading[1].length + 1, 6), inline: parseInline(heading[2]) });
      i += 1;
      continue;
    }

    if (TABLE_RE.test(line)) {
      const rows = [];
      while (i < lines.length && TABLE_RE.test(lines[i])) {
        if (!TABLE_RULE_RE.test(lines[i])) rows.push(cells(lines[i]).map((cell) => parseInline(cell)));
        i += 1;
      }
      const [header, ...rest] = rows;
      blocks.push({ kind: "table", header: header ?? [], rows: rest });
      continue;
    }

    if (LIST_ITEM_RE.test(line)) {
      const items = [];
      const ordered = /\d/.test(line.match(LIST_ITEM_RE)[2]);
      while (i < lines.length && LIST_ITEM_RE.test(lines[i])) {
        items.push({ inline: parseInline(lines[i].match(LIST_ITEM_RE)[3]) });
        i += 1;
      }
      blocks.push({ kind: "list", ordered, items });
      continue;
    }

    const paragraph = [];
    while (
      i < lines.length &&
      lines[i].trim() !== "" &&
      !KEYWORD_RE.test(lines[i]) &&
      !HEADING_RE.test(lines[i]) &&
      !SRC_OPEN_RE.test(lines[i]) &&
      !BLOCK_OPEN_RE.test(lines[i]) &&
      !LIST_ITEM_RE.test(lines[i]) &&
      !TABLE_RE.test(lines[i])
    ) {
      paragraph.push(lines[i]);
      i += 1;
    }
    blocks.push({ kind: "paragraph", inline: parseInline(paragraph.join("\n")) });
  }

  return blocks;
}

function cells(line) {
  let trimmed = line.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith("|")) trimmed = trimmed.slice(1);
  if (trimmed.endsWith("|")) trimmed = trimmed.slice(0, -1);
  return trimmed.split("|").map((cell) => cell.trim());
}

export function parseInline(text) {
  if (!text) return [];
  const out = [];
  let last = 0;
  text.replace(INLINE_RE, (match, _g, offset) => {
    if (offset > last) out.push({ kind: "text", text: text.slice(last, offset) });
    if (match.startsWith("[[")) {
      const link = match.match(/^\[\[([^\]]+?)\](?:\[([^\]]+?)\])?\]$/);
      out.push({ kind: "link", href: link[1], text: link[2] ?? link[1] });
    } else if (match.startsWith("*")) out.push({ kind: "bold", text: match.slice(1, -1) });
    else if (match.startsWith("/")) out.push({ kind: "italic", text: match.slice(1, -1) });
    else if (match.startsWith("_")) out.push({ kind: "italic", text: match.slice(1, -1) });
    else out.push({ kind: "code", text: match.slice(1, -1) });
    last = offset + match.length;
    return match;
  });
  if (last < text.length) out.push({ kind: "text", text: text.slice(last) });
  return out.length ? out : [{ kind: "text", text }];
}
#+end_src

* T1.1 - VDEX harnessed and tooling 

His words: /"ignore the officers for now. we do the office vdex now. not agentic. work out the harness and
tooling of vdex. as well as the boot twitch. propose a full update to vdex here"/, then his comments on
the first cut (below, verbatim, each answered). Officers untouched; no AGENTIC; every file is the vdex
package (=~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/=), the domain's two entity files (a dead method
each), the package's shared scenario and README. Nothing under =systems/= or =subsystems/=.

** his comments — answered

- /"@beef do we want the plain tools on vdex or an application of these tools? i think the latter. vdex
  should provide the functionality that fs tools provide. wrap for context and utility."/ → the =fs_*=
  slurp is gone. The vdex speaks in SLUGS, never paths: =index= · =find= · =read= · =write= · =file= ·
  =move= · =delete= · =open=. Each verb is implemented ONCE, in the aperture (=/document= · =/write= ·
  =/file= · =/move= · =/delete=), where the App already calls it; the tools wrap =ctx.mode.call.<verb>=
  with the model-facing valence, the receipt and the refusal-as-prompt. Three faces, one body.
- /"@beef not really a tool. more of an emitter."/ → yes. =open= is the vdex's one emission:
  =emitter/index.js= =/open= (slug → the App buffer), the mode declares =EMITTER=, the trait binds the
  thread and flushes; the aperture serves it at =/emit/open= (the old =/open= route goes); the tool
  =open= is the receipt over =ctx.mode.emit.open(...)= — the aprende =drill= shape.
- /"i think this is entirely garbage?. idea of boot is to update the literal. but does the literal need
  update? and can he process it? ie are literals subscribed to on the client? if so, we want to be
  precise with our subscription! not just load * when *."/ → the client can subscribe precisely:
  =terminal.daemon.entities.literal.subscribe({ ontology: "vdex" }, callback)= streams each changed ROW
  (op + entity), and the matcher speaks =$like=, so =symbol.subscribe({ slug: { $like: "vdex.%" } })=
  covers the tree. Precision on both sides now: the server syncs PER PATH — a disk event names the file,
  =sync= describes that file and writes ITS row (or removes it), so the ORM emits exactly the rows that
  changed; the App applies each event to the row it names (swap · add · drop by id), refetches nothing,
  and re-reads the open document only when its own hash moved. The full pass exists once: the dataset
  install at boot.
- /"do we want prune on repository level???!?!?! not sure. and for sure not like this. way tooo narrow.
  one ontology key - static?!?! makes no sense and is very out of style. just implement inline for now."/
  → inline in =boot.js=, and through the em — the domain's =prune()= methods were =nativeDelete=, which
  bypasses the ORM subscriber: those removals never reached the twitch. The two methods (vdex was their
  only consumer) are deleted from the domain's =Literal.ts= / =Symbol.ts=.
- /"weird line. very unclear."/ → =boot= reads in three lines with the case named: =drain()= is null when
  the loader's stamp matched and nothing was scanned — the rows already are the tree.
- /"wtf is daemon traits stamp???? what!"/ ×2 → gone, with the whole stamp comparison. Per-path sync needs
  no fingerprint: an unchanged file is an =assign= with no diff — no UPDATE, no event. Our own write's
  disk echo describes the same bytes and mtime the write already stored. The package never touches the
  loader's =installed=.
- /"shouldnt this watch files??!!! this should watch files and twitch changes to files onto literals... or
  is this not needed at all? this code is gibberish!"/ → it watches files now: =Deno.watchFs= on the mount,
  events → the paths they name → =sync(paths)= → those rows → the twitch. A gone path takes every row under
  it (a folder move is remove + create); a file is upserted; a folder is walked and reconciled. Needed:
  without it, an editor, Finder or git changes the JDex and the screen lies until the next boot.

** what is — measured

- The twitch is =daemon.twitch=: the ORM subscriber (=shape.subscriber=) fires =/after/<entity>/<op>= on
  every flush of a managed entity; =shard.datamap.reactive= turns that into =/entities/<type>/subscribe=
  for the client, filtered by =object.match= — the same =where= matcher the client uses, =$like= included.
  kajuit already streams it for =buffer= and =turn= (=focus.js:16=). =RemoteRepository.subscribe(where,
  callback)= merges the row into the identity map and calls back with =(merged, event)=; =event.entity= is
  the wire row, =event.op= ∈ create · update · delete; returns the teardown.
- =nativeDelete= (the domain's =prune=) skips the subscriber — silent to the twitch. Removals must go
  through =em.remove= + =flush=.
- =ctx.mode.call= (EXPOSED) and =ctx.mode.emit= (EMITTER) are bound on the mode's aperture with =daemon=
  and =mode= in production (=resolution.js:39-43=) and in the scenario (=scenarios/mode.js:77-79=); the
  scenario staggers =EMITTER= and =EXPOSED=, so tools calling the aperture and the emitter are testable
  through =shape.object(mode.tools)=.
- =Freight= has =stow= · =admit= · =resolve= · =catalog= — no removal. A deleted document's cargo entry
  stays until the next boot (serves nothing; tangle below).
- FSEvents reports real paths: a mount under =/var= or =/tmp= comes back as =/private/…=; the watcher
  resolves the root once with =Deno.realPath=.
- Harness: =HARNESSED= gives the mode =/harness/dialogue= + =/harness/object=; =system.vdex= is the bare
  index; no role, no doctrine, no notion of what is on the operator's screen.
- The App reaches the client daemon as =terminal.daemon= (=Dojo.svelte:239=).

** landed — six rows on "you do these now", the proof, three corrections

His paste of h1 · e1 · p1 · t1 · b1 · k1 = the go. Written as pasted (his =console.log= in the harness kept;
the =@beef= comment on =OPEN= answered below, not shipped), then PROVEN in the scratchpad against the package
scenario before the report — the proof found three defects in b1 and one outside the package. Each fix is
in the blocks above; the adjustments, named:

- the fold is an UPDATE fact :: =LiteralSubscriber.beforeCreate= writes =symbol= = ={}= for a row created
  and linked in one flush (measured: initialized collection, three items, fold empty; a =symbol= given at
  create is overwritten). =beforeUpdate= folds from the linked collection. So =reconcile= touches every
  FRESH row once (=assign({updatedAt})= + flush) — the dataset installer's own recipe (=linkPhase=). Without
  it every new document's coordinate was an orphan the moment it was minted.
- the m:n cascades REMOVE both ways :: =Literal.symbols= and =Symbol.literals= both carry
  =Cascade.REMOVE= (=kernel/Literal.ts:100=, =Symbol.ts:60=). =em.remove(row)= on a row whose =symbols=
  is initialized (created or populated in the same identity map) took its area · category · format
  symbols with it; =em.remove(orphanSymbol)= with an initialized =literals= took the document. Measured:
  a file → move → delete in one scope wiped =vdex.area.10-19= · =vdex.category.12= · =vdex.format.md=.
  So every pass rides an em FORK (=daemon.entities.em.fork()= + repositories by =getEntityName()=, the
  installer's habit): nothing is initialized but what the pass loads plain; the watcher needs no request
  context; the subscriber is the orm's and twitches for a fork. Landmine ledgered for the runtime (his).
- a row's refs carry no traits :: =load.js row()= names symbols by slug only; the STRUCTURAL + LABELED
  bodies live on =coordinates(path).symbols=. =upsert= minted bare symbols (=traits: []=, =trait: {}=)
  and never followed a folder rename. Now it mints from the coordinates, and a known coordinate whose
  PATH moved takes the folder's name too (a walked folder relabels from NN.00); a coordinate whose path
  did not move keeps the name the index gave it.
- the sibling's =placement= delta folded in (=ownPath=; a rename in place returns the row unmoved).

Proof (scratch =proof/t11.proof.test.js=, not landed — the x-rows carry the real tests): tools index ·
read text · read binary → cargo url · read missing · write · open · open missing · the eight names — PASS;
=/emit/open= over the wire binds the thread, =system.vdex= = role + doctrine + tree + "On the operator's
screen: readme.org · The company (org)." — PASS; the watcher: a dropped file lands with title · fold ·
minted coordinate (path as description), a deleted file leaves with its coordinate, a renamed folder
carries its rows and relabels the category, a path under =bak/= never syncs — PASS; an own write's disk
echo: same hash, same =updatedAt= — PASS; file · move in place · move across · delete, six refusals —

per id — /"i want the full index. not an abbreviation. rows. no indentation format. agent/human
optimized"/. So: every coordinate and every document, one row per line, no indentation, no budget fold.
A coordinate row names a folder (=20-29 koerperschaft= · =22 finance= · =22.04 accounting=); a document
row is =slug · title= — the slug already carries the id and the format, so an agent greps the slug it
will pass to =read= / =open= / =move= and a human reads a list; an id that is ONE file speaks only through
its document row (no =12.04 ubiquity…= folder line above =12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md · …=); a
loose document sits right under its category; =unfiled= closes the list; =· not readable= marks a format
the mode does not serve. The legend rides the header row. =under= narrows to one branch (the tool's
=id=); the depth fold and =budget= are gone — a 2 000-document JDex puts 2 000 rows in the prompt, and
that is the index he asked for; a cap is his to name.

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/index.js — the whole file

#+begin_src js
// the whole JDex as rows, one per line, no indentation — a coordinate row names a folder (`20-29 koerperschaft` ·
// `22 finance` · `22.04 accounting`), a document row is `slug · title`, the slug carrying its id and format
// (`22.04.re24-05277.pdf · RE24-05277`); an id that is one file is only its document row; `not readable` marks a
// format the mode does not serve. Every row stands alone: an agent greps a slug, a human reads a list.
const LEGEND = "rows: a coordinate names a folder · a document is slug · title (the slug carries id and format)";

const named = (labels, slug, key) => `${key} ${labels.get(slug) ?? ""}`.trim();
const line = (literal) => {
  const { title, readable } = literal.trait?.DOCUMENT ?? {};
  return `${literal.slug} · ${title ?? ""}${readable === false ? " · not readable" : ""}`;
};
const bySlug = (a, b) => (a.slug < b.slug ? -1 : 1);

export const index = async (daemon, mode, { under = "" } = {}) => {
  const symbols = await daemon.entities.symbol.find({ slug: { $like: "vdex.%" } });
  const labels = new Map(symbols.map((symbol) => [symbol.slug, symbol.trait?.LABELED?.name ?? ""]));
  const homes = new Map(symbols.map((symbol) => [symbol.slug, symbol.trait?.LABELED?.description ?? null]));
  const literals = await daemon.entities.literal.find({ ontology: "vdex" }, { fields: ["slug", "symbol", "trait"] });
  const areas = {};
  const unfiled = [];
  for (const literal of literals) {
    const at = literal.symbol?.vdex;
    if (!at?.area) {
      unfiled.push(literal);
      continue;
    }
    const category = at.category ?? "";
    const id = at.id ? `${Object.keys(at.id)[0]}.${Object.values(at.id)[0]}` : "";
    if (under && ![at.area, category, id].some((key) => key.startsWith(under))) continue;
    (((areas[at.area] ??= {})[category] ??= {})[id] ??= []).push(literal);
  }
  const rows = [`[VDex — ${mode.mountpoint.absolute}] · ${LEGEND}`];
  for (const area of Object.keys(areas).sort()) {
    rows.push(named(labels, `vdex.area.${area}`, area));
    const categories = areas[area];
    for (const category of Object.keys(categories).sort()) {
      if (category) rows.push(named(labels, `vdex.category.${category}`, category));
      const ids = categories[category];
      for (const id of Object.keys(ids).sort()) {
        const held = ids[id].sort(bySlug);
        // an id folder is a row of its own; an id that is one file speaks through its document row
        const folder = id && !(held.length === 1 && held[0].trait?.DOCUMENT?.asset?.path === homes.get(`vdex.id.${id}`));
        if (folder) rows.push(named(labels, `vdex.id.${id}`, id));
        for (const literal of held) rows.push(line(literal));
      }
    }
  }
  if (unfiled.length && !under) rows.push("unfiled", ...unfiled.sort(bySlug).map(line));
  return rows.join("\n");
};
#+end_src

** h1 — the harness: what the archivist knows

=system.vdex= becomes four sections, blank ones dropped: the ROLE (archivist of the company — the
company from =daemon.statics.company=, as the officers read it), the DOCTRINE (JD in five lines: the
grammar, the NN.00 index, the naming law, "never invent a coordinate", binary vs text, "a write lands on
screen"), the TREE (the index, as now), and the SCREEN — the newest vdex buffer on this thread with a
=data.open=, rendered as one line. The screen line is what makes "summarize this" or "fix the second
paragraph" work without the operator naming the file; it is read from the buffer row, never from the
client. No persona prose beyond the role line — the doctrine is law, not character.

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/harness.js — the whole file

#+begin_src js
import { Vector } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { index } from "./index.js";

const ROLE = (company) =>
  `You are the archivist of ${company.name} (${company.seat}, ${company.stage}). The JDex on the mount is yours: ` +
  "you find, read, file, edit and tidy the company's documents for the operator, and you say where a thing is filed before you touch it.";

const DOCTRINE = [
  "The JDex is Johnny.Decimal: areas NN-NN hold categories NN, categories hold ids NN.NN, and documents live at ids.",
  "NN.00 is the category's index. Folders and files are named `NN.NN Title`; a document is `NN.NN Title.ext` in its category, or any name inside an id folder.",
  "Only the coordinates in the rows below exist — never invent an area, category or id; a new one is the operator's decision.",
  "Documents are addressed by slug — find gives slugs. Text is read with read; binary (pdf, images) is put on screen with open and never read.",
  "A write lands on disk and on the operator's screen at once; there is nothing to refresh.",
].join("\n");

// the newest vdex buffer on this thread that holds a document — what the operator is looking at
const screen = async (ctx) => {
  if (!ctx.input.thread) return null;
  const [buffer] = await ctx.daemon.entities.buffer.find(
    { thread: ctx.input.thread, mode: ctx.mode.entity.id },
    { orderBy: { index: "desc" }, limit: 1 },
  );
  if (!buffer?.data?.open) return null;
  const literal = await ctx.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ ontology: "vdex", slug: buffer.data.open });
  if (!literal) return null;
  const { title, format } = literal.trait.DOCUMENT;
  return `On the operator's screen: ${literal.slug} · ${title} (${format}).`;
};

export const harness = new Vector().use(async (ctx, next) => {
  const sections = [ROLE(ctx.daemon.statics.company), DOCTRINE, await index(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode), await screen(ctx)];
  ctx.hallucination.system.vdex = sections.filter(Boolean).join("\n\n");
  console.log("ctx.hallucination.system:", ctx.hallucination.system);

  await next();
});
#+end_src

The vdex's one emission. The trait casts the input, binds =ctx.thread= from =input.thread=, drains the
pool, stamps =buffer.thread= + =index= and flushes — nothing of that is written here. Reachable three
ways: =mode.emit.open(...)= in process, =/emit/open= over the wire, the tool =open= as a receipt.

#+begin_src js
import { v, Vector } from "@vivalence/typology";

const OPEN = v.object({
  slug: v.string().desc("The document to put on screen."),
  thread: v.string().optional().desc("Binds the emitted buffer to the caller's thread."),
});

export const emitter = new Vector().open({ nature: "/open", input: OPEN }, async (ctx) => {
  const literal = await ctx.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ ontology: "vdex", slug: ctx.input.slug });
  if (!literal) throw new Error(`${ctx.input.slug} is not in the vdex`);
  return ctx.mode.app.buffer({ literals: [literal.id], data: { open: literal.slug } });
});
#+end_src

** p1 — the aperture: the verbs, implemented once

=/index= · =/tree= · =/documents= · =/document= as landed. =/write= syncs the one path it wrote. NEW:
=/file= (id · title · text · format) places by the JD law, =/move= (slug · id · title?) refiles by the
same law and syncs both paths, =/delete= (slug) removes and syncs. =/open= goes — =/emit/open= is the
emission. =placement= is the law in one function: a coordinate's home on disk is read from the
structural symbol boot minted (=LABELED.description= IS the path); an id that is a folder takes the file
inside; an id not yet minted takes =NN.NN Title.ext= in its category and is minted on sync; an id that is
one file already, a category, an area, a missing category, an existing path — each refused with the next
move in the message. A document's OWN path passes (the sibling's delta: a rename in place is =/move= with
the literal's own id). The App keeps calling these; the tools call them too.

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/aperture/index.js — the whole file

#+begin_src js
import { Aperture } from "@vivalence/typology";
import paladin from "@vivalence/paladin";
import { TEXT } from "../types.js";
import { coordinate } from "../jd.js";
import { index } from "../index.js";
import { sync } from "../boot.js";

const COORDINATE = /^vdex\.(area|category|id)\./;
const ID = /^\d\d\.\d\d$/;
const document = (ctx, slug) => ctx.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ ontology: "vdex", slug });
const at = (ctx, path) => paladin.skills.resolve(ctx.mode.mountpoint.absolute, path);
const filename = (title) => title.replace(/[\\/]/g, "-").trim();
const serve = async (ctx, literal) => {
  if (!literal) return null;
  const { asset, format } = literal.trait.DOCUMENT;
  return {
    literal,
    url: ctx.mode.freight.catalog[asset.path]?.url ?? null,
    text: TEXT.includes(format) ? await Deno.readTextFile(at(ctx, asset.path)) : null,
  };
};

// a coordinate's home on disk: the structural symbol's description is the path boot minted it from
const home = async (ctx, slug) => {
  const symbol = await ctx.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug });
  const path = symbol?.trait?.LABELED?.description;
  const stat = path ? await Deno.stat(at(ctx, path)).catch(() => null) : null;
  return stat ? { path, directory: stat.isDirectory } : null;
};

// where a document titled `title` goes at `id`, by the JD law — or why it cannot; every refusal names the next move.
// A document's own path passes (a rename in place keeps its id); any OTHER file at the id still refuses.
const placement = async (ctx, id, title, format, ownPath = null) => {
  if (!ID.test(id))
    throw new Error(`${id} is ${id.includes("-") ? "an area" : "a category"}, not an id — documents file at NN.NN; pick an id under it from the index.`);
  const category = await home(ctx, `vdex.category.${id.split(".")[0]}`);
  if (!category) throw new Error(`there is no category ${id.split(".")[0]} on the mount — see the index; a new category is the operator's to make.`);
  const own = await home(ctx, `vdex.id.${id}`);
  if (own && !own.directory && own.path !== ownPath)
    throw new Error(`${id} is one document already: ${own.path} — write it, or file at a free id from the index.`);
  const path = own?.directory ? `${own.path}/${filename(title)}.${format}` : `${category.path}/${id} ${filename(title)}.${format}`;
  if (path !== ownPath && (await Deno.stat(at(ctx, path)).catch(() => null))) throw new Error(`${path} exists — read it, then write.`);
  return path;
};

export const aperture = new Aperture()
  .open("/index", (input, ctx) => index(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, input))
  .open("/tree", async (input, ctx) =>
    (await ctx.daemon.entities.symbol.find({ slug: { $like: "vdex.%" } })).filter((symbol) => COORDINATE.test(symbol.slug)),
  )
  .open("/documents", (input, ctx) =>
    ctx.daemon.entities.literal.find(
      { ontology: "vdex", ...(input.id && { symbols: [coordinate(input.id)] }) },
      { limit: input.limit ?? 50 },
    ),
  )
  .open("/document", async (input, ctx) => serve(ctx, await document(ctx, input.slug)))
  .open("/write", async (input, ctx) => {
    const literal = await document(ctx, input.slug);
    if (!literal) throw new Error(`${input.slug} is not in the vdex`);
    const { path } = literal.trait.DOCUMENT.asset;
    await Deno.writeTextFile(at(ctx, path), input.text);
    const { literal: [written] } = await sync(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, [path]);
    return serve(ctx, written);
  })
  .open("/file", async (input, ctx) => {
    const format = input.format ?? "md";
    if (!TEXT.includes(format)) throw new Error(`${format} is not a text format — one of ${TEXT.join(", ")}; binary arrives on the mount, not through here.`);
    const path = await placement(ctx, input.id, input.title, format);
    await Deno.writeTextFile(at(ctx, path), input.text ?? "");
    const { literal: [literal] } = await sync(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, [path]);
    return serve(ctx, literal);
  })
  .open("/move", async (input, ctx) => {
    const literal = await document(ctx, input.slug);
    if (!literal) throw new Error(`${input.slug} is not in the vdex`);
    const { asset, format, title } = literal.trait.DOCUMENT;
    const to = await placement(ctx, input.id, input.title ?? title, format, asset.path);
    if (to === asset.path) return serve(ctx, literal);
    await Deno.rename(at(ctx, asset.path), at(ctx, to));
    const { literal: [moved] } = await sync(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, [asset.path, to]);
    return serve(ctx, moved);
  })
  .open("/delete", async (input, ctx) => {
    const literal = await document(ctx, input.slug);
    if (!literal) throw new Error(`${input.slug} is not in the vdex`);
    const { path } = literal.trait.DOCUMENT.asset;
    await Deno.remove(at(ctx, path));
    await sync(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, [path]);
    return { slug: input.slug, path };
  });
#+end_src

** t1 — the tooling: an application of the verbs

Slug-addressed, one implementation behind each. =index= (the tree with counts, narrowed on =id=) ·
=find= (coordinate · title · slug → slugs; the query is the literal repository's own) · =read= (slug →
text, capped at =head= with a "read again with a larger head" trailer; binary → "put it on screen with
open" + the cargo url) · =write= (slug · text) · =file= (id · title · text · format) · =move= (slug · id ·
title?) · =delete= (slug) · =open= (slug → the emitter, receipt back). An aperture refusal (a thrown
Error) comes back as =condition: "ERROR"= with the message — errors are prompts. Names in the vdex's own
harness are these eight, bare.

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/tools/index.js — the whole file

#+begin_src js
import { v, Vector } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { TEXT } from "../types.js";
import { coordinate } from "../jd.js";

const HEAD = 12000;
const line = (literal) => {
  const { title, format, readable } = literal.trait.DOCUMENT;
  return `${literal.slug} · ${title} · ${format}${readable ? "" : " · not readable"}`;
};
const refuse = (message) => ({ condition: "ERROR", output: { message } });
// an aperture refusal is a thrown Error whose message already names the next move
const attempt = async (run) => {
  try {
    return await run();
  } catch (error) {
    return refuse(error.message);
  }
};

export const tools = new Vector()
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/index",
      valence:
        "The filing tree — areas, categories and ids with document counts and formats. Narrow to a coordinate " +
        "to see deeper. Read it after your own writes; the tree in your instructions is from the start of the turn.",
      input: v.object({ id: v.string().optional().desc("A JD coordinate to narrow to: 22, 22.04, 20-29.") }),
    },
    async (ctx) => ({ output: { message: await ctx.mode.call.index({ under: ctx.input.id ?? "" }) } }),
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/find",
      valence:
        "Find documents by JD coordinate, title or slug — the slugs every other verb takes. Resolve a coordinate " +
        "to its documents before reading. Binary formats (pdf, images) are listed and opened on screen, never read.",
      input: v.object({
        id: v.string().optional().desc("A JD coordinate: 22, 22.04, 20-29."),
        text: v.string().optional().desc("Matches slug and title."),
        limit: v.integer({ minimum: 1, maximum: 50 }).default(20),
      }),
    },
    async (ctx) => {
      const where = { ontology: "vdex" };
      if (ctx.input.id) where.symbols = [coordinate(ctx.input.id)];
      if (ctx.input.text) where.search = ctx.input.text;
      const literals = await ctx.daemon.entities.literal.find(where, { limit: ctx.input.limit });
      return { output: { message: literals.map(line).join("\n") || "nothing filed there.", literal: literals } };
    },
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/read",
      valence: "Read a document's text by slug. Binary formats are not read — put them on screen with open.",
      input: v.object({
        slug: v.string().desc("From find."),
        head: v.integer({ minimum: 1000, maximum: 60000 }).default(HEAD).desc("Characters to return from the start."),
      }),
    },
    (ctx) =>
      attempt(async () => {
        const served = await ctx.mode.call.document({ slug: ctx.input.slug });
        if (!served) return refuse(`${ctx.input.slug} is not in the vdex — find first.`);
        const { title, format } = served.literal.trait.DOCUMENT;
        if (served.text === null)
          return {
            output: {
              message: `${served.literal.slug} · ${title} is ${format} — binary; put it on screen with open${served.url ? ` (cargo: ${served.url})` : ""}.`,
              literal: [served.literal],
            },
          };
        const text =
          served.text.length > ctx.input.head
            ? `${served.text.slice(0, ctx.input.head)}\n… (${served.text.length - ctx.input.head} more characters — read again with a larger head)`
            : served.text;
        return { output: { message: `${served.literal.slug} · ${title} · ${format}\n\n${text}`, literal: [served.literal] } };
      }),
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/write",
      valence: "Replace a document's text by slug. It stays where it is filed; the operator's screen follows.",
      input: v.object({ slug: v.string().desc("From find."), text: v.string().desc("The whole new text.") }),
    },
    (ctx) =>
      attempt(async () => {
        const served = await ctx.mode.call.write({ slug: ctx.input.slug, text: ctx.input.text });
        return { output: { message: `wrote ${served.text.length} characters to ${served.literal.slug}`, literal: [served.literal] } };
      }),
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/file",
      valence:
        "File a NEW text document at an id (NN.NN) — the JD naming is done for you. An id that is a folder takes the " +
        "file inside it; an id not yet minted takes `NN.NN Title.ext` in its category. Existing documents are never " +
        "overwritten: read them, then write.",
      input: v.object({
        id: v.string().desc("The id to file at: 22.04. Areas and categories are folders — documents live at ids."),
        title: v.string().desc("The title; becomes the file name."),
        text: v.string().desc("The document's text."),
        format: v.enum(TEXT).default("md"),
      }),
    },
    (ctx) =>
      attempt(async () => {
        const served = await ctx.mode.call.file(ctx.input);
        return { output: { message: `filed ${served.literal.trait.DOCUMENT.asset.path} as ${served.literal.slug}`, literal: [served.literal] } };
      }),
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/move",
      valence: "Refile a document at another id, optionally under a new title. The slug changes with the coordinate — use the one returned.",
      input: v.object({
        slug: v.string().desc("From find."),
        id: v.string().desc("The id to refile at: 22.04."),
        title: v.string().optional().desc("A new title; the current one otherwise."),
      }),
    },
    (ctx) =>
      attempt(async () => {
        const served = await ctx.mode.call.move(ctx.input);
        return {
          output: { message: `${ctx.input.slug} is now ${served.literal.slug} at ${served.literal.trait.DOCUMENT.asset.path}`, literal: [served.literal] },
        };
      }),
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/delete",
      valence: "Delete a document by slug — gone from disk and from the tree; there is no undo.",
      input: v.object({ slug: v.string().desc("From find.") }),
    },
    (ctx) =>
      attempt(async () => {
        const gone = await ctx.mode.call.delete({ slug: ctx.input.slug });
        return { output: { message: `deleted ${gone.slug} (${gone.path})` } };
      }),
  )
  .open(
    {
      nature: "/open",
      valence: "Put a document on the operator's screen — the reader opens it in the VDex app.",
      input: v.object({ slug: v.string().desc("From find.") }),
    },
    async (ctx) => {
      const literal = await ctx.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ ontology: "vdex", slug: ctx.input.slug });
      if (!literal) return refuse(`${ctx.input.slug} is not in the vdex — find first.`);
      const emission = await ctx.mode.emit.open({ slug: literal.slug, thread: ctx.thread });
      return { output: { message: `${literal.slug} on screen.`, ...emission.output } };
    },
  );
#+end_src

** b1 — the boot twitch: boot · prune · sync per path · watch

=boot=: the dataset trait already installed the tree as rows; =drain()= hands over that scan, or null
when the loader's stamp matched and nothing was scanned (the rows already are the tree). Prune inline
through the em: rows whose file the scan did not see, then coordinates no row sits under (read off the
rows' folded =symbol.vdex=). Then the watcher; its teardown is boot's return.

=sync(daemon, mode, paths)=: the one mechanism every change rides — the aperture verbs pass the path
they touched, the watcher passes the paths the disk named. Removals first (a gone path takes every row
under it; a folder walk removes what the folder no longer holds), one flush; then upserts, one flush —
so a row re-created under a slug just freed never trips the unique slug. =upsert= makes the row exactly
as boot would (=row()= from =load.js=, same title · hash · symbols): a known path keeps its slug, a new
path takes a free one against the OTHER rows' slugs; coordinate symbols are reused or minted and linked;
an NN.00 index names its category (=label=, now exported). Unchanged content is an =assign= with no
diff: no UPDATE, no event — which is why our own write's disk echo costs one stat and one read and emits
nothing. Orphaned coordinates go after every sync.

=watch=: =Deno.watchFs= on the real root, ignore list shared with the index walk, the paths of a burst
collected for 250 ms into one =sync=. Every =sync= — an aperture verb's or the watcher's — waits its turn
on one chain, and every pass rides its own em FORK (the dataset installer's habit): a clean identity map,
no request context needed, the ORM subscriber (the twitch) sees every flush.

#+begin_src js
import { extname } from "@std/path";
import { object } from "@vivalence/typology";
import paladin from "@vivalence/paladin";
import { drain, row, label } from "./load.js";
import { coordinates } from "./jd.js";

const COORDINATE = /^vdex\.(area|category|id)\./;
const SETTLE = 250;

// every pass rides its own em fork with a clean identity map — the dataset installer's habit. The literal↔symbol
// m:n cascades REMOVE both ways over whatever is loaded, so a row removed through the caller's em could take its
// coordinates with it (or a coordinate its documents); a fork holds nothing but what the pass itself loaded.
// The ORM subscriber is the orm's, not the em's: the twitch sees every write a fork flushes.
const fork = (daemon) => {
  const em = daemon.entities.em.fork();
  return { em, literal: em.getRepository(daemon.entities.literal.getEntityName()), symbol: em.getRepository(daemon.entities.symbol.getEntityName()) };
};

const rows = (store, fields) => store.literal.find({ ontology: "vdex" }, fields ? { fields } : {});
const structural = async (store) =>
  (await store.symbol.find({ slug: { $like: "vdex.%" } })).filter((symbol) => COORDINATE.test(symbol.slug));

// coordinates no document sits under — read off the rows' folded symbol.vdex
const orphaned = async (store) => {
  const used = new Set();
  for (const { symbol } of await rows(store, ["symbol"])) {
    const { area, category, id } = symbol?.vdex ?? {};
    if (area) used.add(`vdex.area.${area}`);
    if (category) used.add(`vdex.category.${category}`);
    if (id) used.add(`vdex.id.${Object.entries(id)[0].join(".")}`);
  }
  return (await structural(store)).filter((symbol) => !used.has(symbol.slug));
};

// every removal goes through the em: the twitch carries only what the subscriber saw
const removeOrphans = async (store) => {
  const orphans = await orphaned(store);
  for (const symbol of orphans) store.em.remove(symbol);
  await store.em.flush();
  return orphans.length;
};

// what the tree no longer has: rows whose file the scan did not see, then coordinates left without a document
const prune = async (store, fresh) => {
  const scanned = new Set(fresh.literal.map((held) => held.slug));
  const gone = (await rows(store)).filter((held) => !scanned.has(held.slug));
  for (const held of gone) store.em.remove(held);
  await store.em.flush();
  return { literal: gone.length, symbol: await removeOrphans(store) };
};

const report = async (store, mode, pruned) =>
  console.log(
    `[VDEX] ${mode.mountpoint.absolute} ${await store.literal.count({ ontology: "vdex" })} documents · ` +
      `${(await structural(store)).length} coordinates · pruned ${pruned.literal}/${pruned.symbol}`,
  );

// the dataset trait installed the tree as rows before boot. drain() hands over that scan — or null when the
// loader's stamp matched and nothing was scanned, in which case the rows already are the tree and there is
// nothing to prune against. Then the watcher; its teardown is boot's return, owed back at disintegrate.
export async function boot(daemon, mode) {
  const store = fork(daemon);
  const fresh = await drain(mode);
  const pruned = fresh ? await prune(store, fresh) : { literal: 0, symbol: 0 };
  await report(store, mode, pruned);
  return watch(daemon, mode);
}

const describe = async (root, path) => {
  const stat = await Deno.stat(`${root}/${path}`).catch(() => null);
  if (!stat) return null;
  if (stat.isDirectory) return { path, directory: true };
  return { path, format: extname(path).slice(1).toLowerCase(), bytes: stat.size, mtime: stat.mtime?.toISOString() ?? null };
};

// one file → its row, as boot would have made it: a known path keeps its slug, a new one takes a free slug
// against the other rows; its coordinates are reused or minted and linked; an NN.00 index names its category.
// Unchanged content is an assign with no diff — no UPDATE, no event.
const upsert = async (store, mode, file, known, others) => {
  const coordinate = coordinates(file.path);
  const fresh = await row(mode, file, coordinate, new Set(others.map((held) => held.slug)));
  if (known) fresh.slug = known.slug;
  // the row names its symbols by slug; the coordinates carry their traits (the structural symbols jd minted)
  const refs = new Map(fresh.symbols.map((ref) => [ref.slug, ref]));
  for (const symbol of coordinate.symbols) if (symbol.traits) refs.set(symbol.slug, symbol);
  const slugs = [...refs.keys()];
  const minted = new Map((await store.symbol.find({ slug: { $in: slugs } })).map((symbol) => [symbol.slug, symbol]));
  for (const ref of refs.values()) {
    const held = minted.get(ref.slug);
    if (!held) minted.set(ref.slug, store.symbol.create(ref));
    // a known coordinate follows a move: its path and the folder's name (a walked folder relabels from NN.00);
    // a coordinate whose path did not move keeps its name — the NN.00 index set it
    else if (ref.trait?.LABELED && held.trait?.LABELED?.description !== ref.trait.LABELED.description)
      held.trait = { ...held.trait, LABELED: { ...held.trait.LABELED, ...ref.trait.LABELED } };
  }
  label(minted, coordinate, fresh.trait.DOCUMENT.title);
  const data = object.omit(fresh, ["symbols"]);
  const literal = known
    ? await store.literal.findOne({ ontology: "vdex", slug: known.slug }, { populate: ["symbols"] })
    : store.literal.create(data);
  if (known) literal.assign(object.patch(literal, data));
  for (const slug of slugs) literal.symbols.add(minted.get(slug));
  mode.freight.admit(file.path);
  return literal;
};

// the paths that changed → their rows, and only theirs. Removals first (a gone path takes every row under it;
// a folder walk removes what the folder no longer holds), then the upserts, then the coordinates left without
// a document. Returns the rows it wrote and what it removed.
const reconcile = async (daemon, mode, paths) => {
  const root = mode.mountpoint.absolute;
  const store = fork(daemon);
  const em = store.em;
  const held = await rows(store);
  const pathOf = (row) => row.trait.DOCUMENT.asset.path;
  const under = (path) => held.filter((row) => pathOf(row) === path || pathOf(row).startsWith(`${path}/`));
  const gone = new Set();
  const files = [];
  for (const path of paths) {
    const file = await describe(root, path);
    if (!file) for (const row of under(path)) gone.add(row);
    else if (file.directory) {
      const found = (await paladin.find.index(`${root}/${path}`, mode.statics.ignore)).map((inner) => ({ ...inner, path: `${path}/${inner.path}` }));
      const present = new Set(found.map((inner) => inner.path));
      for (const row of under(path)) if (!present.has(pathOf(row))) gone.add(row);
      files.push(...found);
    } else files.push(file);
  }
  for (const row of gone) em.remove(row);
  await em.flush();
  const kept = held.filter((row) => !gone.has(row));
  const literal = [];
  const created = [];
  for (const file of files) {
    const known = kept.find((row) => pathOf(row) === file.path);
    const row = await upsert(store, mode, file, known, kept.filter((row) => row !== known));
    if (!known) created.push(row);
    literal.push(row);
  }
  await em.flush();
  // the subscriber folds symbol.vdex from the linked collection on UPDATE, never at create — a fresh row is
  // touched once so its coordinates count (the dataset installer links the same way)
  for (const row of created) row.assign({ updatedAt: new Date() });
  await em.flush();
  return { literal, removed: { literal: gone.size, symbol: await removeOrphans(store) } };
};

// one sync at a time, whoever asks — an aperture verb's or the watcher's — so our own write's disk echo finds
// the row the verb already wrote and assigns without a diff.
let chain = Promise.resolve();
export const sync = (daemon, mode, paths) => (chain = chain.catch(() => {}).then(() => reconcile(daemon, mode, paths)));

// disk events → the paths they name → one sync per burst. FSEvents reports real paths (/private/var, not
// /var), so the root is resolved once. Returns the teardown.
export const watch = async (daemon, mode) => {
  const root = await Deno.realPath(mode.mountpoint.absolute);
  const ignored = paladin.find.skip(mode.statics.ignore);
  const watcher = Deno.watchFs(root, { recursive: true });
  const pending = new Set();
  let timer = null;
  const settle = () => {
    const paths = [...pending];
    pending.clear();
    sync(daemon, mode, paths).catch((error) => console.error("[VDEX:watch]", error));
  };
  (async () => {
    try {
      for await (const event of watcher) {
        for (const absolute of event.paths) {
          const path = absolute.slice(root.length + 1);
          if (path && !path.split("/").some(ignored)) pending.add(path);
        }
        if (!pending.size) continue;
        clearTimeout(timer);
        timer = setTimeout(settle, SETTLE);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (!(error instanceof Deno.errors.BadResource)) console.error("[VDEX:watch]", error);
    }
  })();
  return () => {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    watcher.close();
  };
};
#+end_src

** a1 — the App: two subscriptions, per row

=literal= under =ontology: "vdex"= and =symbol= under =slug $like vdex.%=. Each event names its row:
swapped, added or dropped by id — =documents= and =symbols= never refetched. The open document re-reads
only when its own row's hash moved and it is not being edited; a deleted open document closes. =save()=
loses its documents refetch — the twitch is that refetch. Leaves sort by slug so a swapped row does not
fall to the end of its list.

#+begin_src js
import { specimen, shape, Url } from "@vivalence/typology";
import * as runtime from "@vivalence/runtime";
import { office, jdex } from "../../../../tests/scenarios.js";
import * as vdex from "../vdex.viva.js";
import { boot } from "../boot.js";

specimen.describe("vdex tools", () => {
  let scenario, root, tools, teardown;

  specimen.beforeAll(async () => {
    root = await jdex();
    scenario = await office(vdex, { mountpoint: root });
    scenario.mode.freight.withUrl(new Url("http://test/attached/cargo/daemon/test-daemon"));
    await scenario.scoped(() => runtime.daemon.traits.DATASET(scenario.mode, scenario.daemon));
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      teardown = await boot(scenario.daemon, scenario.mode);
    });
    tools = shape.object(scenario.mode.tools);
  });

  specimen.afterAll(async () => {
    await teardown?.();
    await scenario?.orm?.close();
    await Deno.remove(root, { recursive: true });
  });

  specimen.it("index renders the tree for the model, narrowed on ask", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const whole = await tools.index({});
      specimen.expect(whole.output.message).toContain("22 Finance\n22.00.finance-index.md · Finance\n22.04 accounting\n22.04.re24-05277.pdf · RE24-05277");
      specimen.expect(whole.output.message).toContain("12 goals");
      const narrowed = await tools.index({ id: "22" });
      specimen.expect(narrowed.output.message).toContain("22.04.re24-05277.pdf");
      specimen.expect(narrowed.output.message).not.toContain("12.04");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("find resolves a category, an id, an area, and text", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ id: "22" })).output.literal.map((row) => row.slug).sort()).toEqual(["22.00.finance-index.md", "22.04.re24-05277.pdf"]);
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ id: "22.04" })).output.literal.map((row) => row.slug)).toEqual(["22.04.re24-05277.pdf"]);
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ id: "10-19" })).output.literal.length).toBe(2);
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ text: "ubiquity" })).output.message).toContain("12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md");
      specimen.expect((await tools.find({ text: "nothing-here" })).output.message).toBe("nothing filed there.");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("read speaks the text by slug; a pdf answers binary with its cargo url", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const text = await tools.read({ slug: "readme.org" });
      specimen.expect(text.output.message).toContain("#+TITLE: The company");
      const binary = await tools.read({ slug: "22.04.re24-05277.pdf" });
      specimen.expect(binary.output.message).toContain("binary");
      specimen.expect(binary.output.message).toContain("/attached/cargo/daemon/test-daemon/20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Rechnungen/RE24-05277.pdf");
      specimen.expect((await tools.read({ slug: "nope.md" })).condition).toBe("ERROR");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("write replaces the text; the row's hash moves, the slug stays", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const before = (await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.notes.md" })).trait.DOCUMENT.hash;
      const written = await tools.write({ slug: "12.notes.md", text: "loose notes, rewritten\n" });
      specimen.expect(written.output.literal[0].slug).toBe("12.notes.md");
      specimen.expect(written.output.literal[0].trait.DOCUMENT.hash).not.toBe(before);
      specimen.expect(await Deno.readTextFile(`${root}/10-19_viva/12_goals/notes.md`)).toBe("loose notes, rewritten\n");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("file names the document by the JD law and refuses what is not a free id", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const inside = await tools.file({ id: "22.04", title: "Notes on invoices", text: "# Notes\n" });
      specimen.expect(inside.output.literal[0].trait.DOCUMENT.asset.path).toBe("20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.04_accounting/Notes on invoices.md");
      specimen.expect(inside.output.literal[0].symbol.vdex.id).toEqual({ "22": "04" });
      const minted = await tools.file({ id: "22.07", title: "Payroll", text: "# Payroll\n", format: "org" });
      specimen.expect(minted.output.literal[0].trait.DOCUMENT.asset.path).toBe("20-29_koerperschaft/22_finance/22.07 Payroll.org");
      specimen.expect(minted.output.literal[0].slug).toBe("22.07.payroll.org");
      specimen.expect((await scenario.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug: "vdex.id.22.07" })).trait.LABELED.name).toBe("payroll");
      specimen.expect((await tools.file({ id: "22", title: "x", text: "" })).output.message).toContain("a category, not an id");
      specimen.expect((await tools.file({ id: "12.04", title: "x", text: "" })).output.message).toContain("one document already");
      specimen.expect((await tools.file({ id: "22.07", title: "Payroll", text: "", format: "org" })).output.message).toContain("exists");
      specimen.expect((await tools.file({ id: "44.01", title: "x", text: "" })).output.message).toContain("no category 44");
    });
  });

  specimen.it("move refiles under another id; delete takes the file, the row and the orphaned coordinate", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
      const moved = await tools.move({ slug: "12.notes.md", id: "12.05", title: "Notes" });
      specimen.expect(moved.output.literal[0].trait.DOCUMENT.asset.path).toBe("10-19_viva/12_goals/12.05 Notes.md");
      specimen.expect(moved.output.literal[0].slug).toBe("12.05.notes.md");
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.notes.md" })).toBeNull();
      specimen.expect(await Deno.stat(`${root}/10-19_viva/12_goals/notes.md`).catch(() => null)).toBeNull();
      const gone = await tools.delete({ slug: "12.05.notes.md" });
      specimen.expect(gone.output.message).toContain("deleted 12.05.notes.md");
      specimen.expect(await Deno.stat(`${root}/10-19_viva/12_goals/12.05 Notes.md`).catch(() => null)).toBeNull();
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ slug: "12.05.notes.md" })).toBeNull();
      specimen.expect(await scenario.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug: "vdex.id.12.05" })).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  specimen.it("open puts the document on screen as a buffer", async () => {
    await scenario.scoped(async () => {
