*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/README.md — hunk

#+begin_src diff
-**`modes/office/`** — `vdex` · `email` · `chat`. Each owns its literals, syncs them from the world on boot, and carries its own scheme symbols.
+**`modes/office/`** — `vdex` · `email` · `chat`. Each owns its literals, syncs them from the world on boot, and carries its own scheme symbols. The vdex watches its mount: a file dropped, edited, moved or removed on disk is its row and a screen update within a second; a write from the screen or a tool is on disk at once. Its verbs — read · write · file · move · delete — are slug-addressed and implemented once in the aperture; the tools and the app both speak them.
#+end_src

** gates

- G1 — the vdex suites: =deno test --config deno.jsonc -A --no-check ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/tests/= (23 → 30 steps); =vdex-hallucination= · =vdex-tools= · =vdex-aperture= recaptured, diffs read; typology · runtime · paladin untouched — no re-run owed.
- G2 — live, after his restart: drop a file into the JDex from Finder → the tree shows it without a reload; edit an open document in an editor → the reader follows; rename a folder → the rows follow; save on screen → one row event, no second =[VDEX]= line.
- G3 — live chat in vdex mode: /"what is on my screen"/ answers from the screen line; /"file a note under 22.04 about X"/ → =file= → the tree shows it; /"put the finance index on screen"/ → =open=; /"move it to 22.07"/ → =move= with the new slug in the receipt.

** tangles

- =Freight= has no removal: a deleted document's cargo entry stays in the catalog until the next boot (it serves nothing — the file is gone). A =Freight.evict(path)= is typology's, holy, a later ask; =sync= would call it where it =admit=s.
- A renamed FILE is remove + create: the new row has a new slug (the slug encodes the coordinate and name — identity by the record's law, m44). A buffer holding the old slug in =data.open= shows the reader's "not in the vdex" until the operator opens the new one; the App closes it on the delete event.
- FSEvents batches a folder rename as events on the folder, not on its files — the directory branch of =sync= walks it; latency ~100–500 ms, inside the 3 s the tests allow.
- =daemon.entities.symbol.create(ref)= for a NEW coordinate symbol relies on =repo.create= persisting on flush (the install's =store.create= relies on the same).
- The harness's screen line reads the NEWEST buffer by =index=; a thread whose last vdex buffer holds a deleted document gets no line (the literal lookup fails), never a stale one.

* T1.B — the vdex as a dataset and a sink (beef's alternative, worked out)
Beef: /"could we maybe accomplish this with just dataset and datasink? … arbitrary loader … patch mode onto dataset literals by default. remove mode ownership on sink tho. we'd still need to prune on boot … twitches on literal level … a filesystem watcher"/. This section is that shape, patch-ready, as an ALTERNATIVE to T1's BOOTED =boot()=: the JDex is a DATASET whose literal source is a loader over the tree, the mode OWNS its rows and DATASET prunes what it no longer declares, a DATASINK writes a document's body back to its file, and BOOTED keeps only the watcher. Everything else in T1 (=jd.js= · =title.js= · =index.js= · =harness.js= · tools · aperture · App · drapes · paladin T1.b) stands unchanged. His call which lands; rollout step 8. ** entities — what changes against T1 - =DOCUMENT.body= rides the row for TEXT formats under =BODY= (256 kB); binary and over-cap rows carry none. The sink has something to write; the App edits the entity, never the disk. - owner rows =mode_literals= · =mode_symbols= (T1.a) are REQUIRED: every dataset row of every DATASET mode gets its installer as owner in the upsert; education's rows gain owners at their next reinstall (=stamp()= folds the installer source, so the =dataset.js= change reinstalls every DATASET mode once). - =Mode.installed= = the loader's fingerprint (paths · bytes · mtimes, no hashes) folded with the scheme file — an unchanged tree boots without touching the index; a changed tree reinstalls and prunes. - the twitches are the ones that exist: =/after/literal/update= (=shape.subscriber= → =daemon.twitch=) wakes the sink; the sink writes ONE file per dirty row. - prune is generic and co-owner safe: a row loses THIS mode's ownership; it is deleted only when no owner remains (two office modes may declare the same scheme symbol). #+begin_src javascript // a filed md — the body rides the row { slug: "12.04.ubiquity-and-a-thin-slice.md", ontology: "vdex", traits: ["DOCUMENT"], trait: { DOCUMENT: { asset: { path: "10-19_viva/12_goals/12.04 Ubiquity and a Thin Slice.md" }, title: "Ubiquity and a Thin Slice", format: "md", readable: true, bytes: 11310, mtime: "…", hash: "0c4f91aa", body: "# Ubiquity and a Thin Slice\n\n…" } }, symbols: [...], modes: [<office/vdex>] } // a pdf — no body, the cargo url is the way in { slug: "22.04.re24-05277.pdf", …, trait: { DOCUMENT: { …, body: undefined } }, modes: [<office/vdex>] } #+end_src ** the mechanism #+begin_example disk ──(DATASET · boot · only when the fingerprint moved)──▶ rows (+ owner)  ──▶ prune(owner ∧ ∉ fresh) rows ──(DATASINK · twitch /after/literal/update · 1.5 s debounce · one file per dirty row)──▶ disk      scribe() compares first — no loop disk ──(BOOTED · Deno.watchFs · 500 ms settle · T1.d)──▶ reindex ──▶ rows (the whole tree, one way)      own writes reindex explicitly too #+end_example The stamp: =resolution.js:49-56= compares =installed= to =stamp(mode)= BEFORE stagger and blanks it when they differ; DATASET then pulls the loaders; a loader source contributes its own =stamp(mode)= to the fold. After a sink write the datasink re-stamps (=datasink.js:62-65=, unchanged) so the next boot does not reinstall for a change the rows already carry; the row's =hash= stays stale until the watcher's =reindex= (or the next foreign change) — accepted. ** files *** subsystems/typology/prototypes/dataset.js landed in =* T1= (c1 — the =reader.load= hunk beside b3's MIME table). His WEIRD moved it up: the loader is the vdex's shape in EITHER design; nothing further here. *** subsystems/typology/prototypes/datasink.js edit — GATED (typology). A per-row writer: =each(row) → path= · =body(row) → text | null= · =root= ("module" | "mountpoint"). Not a target in the collision check (like =split=); one stratum per row. #+begin_src diff split: (pattern, target, keep) => ({ ...writer.lift(target, keep), split: pattern }), +  each: (path, body, root = "module") => ({ each: path, body, root, codec: "text", keep: AUTHORED }), lift: (declared, keep, named) => {@@ get targets() {return this.sinks .map((sink) => sink.target) -      .filter((target) => !target.split) +      .filter((target) => !target.split && !target.each) .map((target) => target.write);} @@ static strata(rows, target) {+    if (target.each) return new Map(rows.map((row) => [target.each(row), [row]])); if (!target.split) return new Map([[target.write, rows]]); #+end_src *** subsystems/typology/schematics/entities/mode.js · literal.js · symbol.js edit — GATED (typology, T1.a). scenario = await office(vdex, { mountpoint: root }); await scenario.scoped(() => runtime.daemon.traits.DATASET(scenario.mode, scenario.daemon)); sink = runtime.daemon.traits.DATASINK(scenario.mode, scenario.daemon); rows byte-identical otherwise deno test --config deno.jsonc -A ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/tests/ viva instances/use vivalence run                                                                   # [DATASET:install] office/vdex … N rows · prune:literal 0 · no [VDEX] index line; [VDEX] watching <mount> viva instances/use vivalence run                                                                   # again, tree unchanged: no [DATASET:install] for the vdex at all echo "# late" > ~/.viva/instances/vivalence/jdex/30-39_funding/30_applications/30.01_content/late.md   # → within a second: the row exists; kajuit sees it without a reboot #+end_src Gates: education's italian boot after the reinstall — rows and log lines byte-identical apart from the one =prune:… 0= line and the owners · a co-owned scheme symbol survives one mode's prune · the sink never re-fires after its own write (=scribe= compared) · the watcher's teardown at =terminate= closes the handle (no leaked =watchFs= in the tests' sanitizers). ** what this section costs and buys Costs: five typology files (holy — =dataset.js= · =datasink.js= · three schematics), three runtime entities, two runtime traits, the datamap pivots; T1.a stops being optional. Buys: the vdex has no boot index (an unchanged tree boots in the time of one stat walk), one write path (entity → sink → disk), generic ownership and prune for EVERY dataset mode, and a live mirror of the JDex. Not measured: =Deno.watchFs= behavior on macOS for a rename (two events, both paths — =reindex= handles each: one disappears, one appears) · =Collection.contains= on a =getReference= owner across a forked =em= · =repo.find({ modes: id })= on the inverse side of an =owner: true= m:n (mikro joins the pivot; the same shape =symbols: this.reference(s)= already uses).
* T2 — office messaging

** entities

*** ontology

=message= — the TOPOGRAPHICAL symbol; =Literal.ontology = "message"= carried explicitly by the sync row, derived by the subscriber.

*** symbols

The scheme (T0, declared by email AND chat through =modes/office/message.js=). No symbols
minted at sync in v1 — senders and threads are trait fields; a =message.sender.<hash>=
dimension is a later fold if "everything from X" needs a set.

*** traits

#+begin_src javascript
MESSAGE: { channel: "email", direction: "in", from: "notariat@example.de", to: ["office@vivalence.org"],
           at: "2026-09-02T08:14:03.000Z", body: "Sehr geehrte Damen und Herren, anbei die Rechnung …",
           external: { id: "<20260902081403.1a2b@example.de>" } }
MAIL:  { subject: "Rechnung RE24-05277", cc: [], messageId: "<20260902081403.1a2b@example.de>", inReplyTo: null, references: [],
           attachments: [{ path: "attachments/email.5f3a9c1e/RE24-05277.pdf", type: "application/pdf", bytes: 48211 }] }
CHAT: { platform: "telegram", chat: "4521", sender: { id: "4521", name: "beef" }, replyTo: null }
#+end_src

*** literals

Slug = =<channel>.<external id, hashed short>=: =email.5f3a9c1e= (Message-ID) · =telegram.4521.918= (chat.message).

#+begin_src javascript
// inbound mail
{ slug: "email.5f3a9c1e", ontology: "message", traits: ["MESSAGE", "MAIL"],
  trait: { MESSAGE: { channel: "email", direction: "in", from: "notariat@example.de", to: ["office@vivalence.org"], at: "2026-09-02T08:14:03.000Z", body: "…", external: { id: "<20260902081403.1a2b@example.de>" } },
           MAIL: { subject: "Rechnung RE24-05277", cc: [], messageId: "<20260902081403.1a2b@example.de>", inReplyTo: null, references: [], attachments: [{ path: "attachments/email.5f3a9c1e/RE24-05277.pdf", type: "application/pdf", bytes: 48211 }] } },
  symbols: [{ slug: "message" }, { slug: "message.channel.email" }, { slug: "message.direction.in" }, { slug: "message.status.unread" }] }

// the reply the chief sent through email_send — `uses` links it to its parent, status on the parent flips to answered
{ slug: "email.9d02b7aa", ontology: "message", traits: ["MESSAGE", "MAIL"],
  trait: { MESSAGE: { channel: "email", direction: "out", from: "office@vivalence.org", to: ["notariat@example.de"], at: "2026-09-02T09:01:12.000Z", body: "Vielen Dank, die Rechnung ist eingegangen …", external: { id: "<…@vivalence.org>" } },
           MAIL: { subject: "Re: Rechnung RE24-05277", cc: [], messageId: "<…@vivalence.org>", inReplyTo: "<20260902081403.1a2b@example.de>", references: ["<20260902081403.1a2b@example.de>"], attachments: [] } },
  symbols: [{ slug: "message" }, { slug: "message.channel.email" }, { slug: "message.direction.out" }, { slug: "message.status.read" }],
  uses: [{ slug: "email.5f3a9c1e" }] }

// a telegram DM
{ slug: "telegram.4521.918", ontology: "message", traits: ["MESSAGE", "CHAT"],
  trait: { MESSAGE: { channel: "telegram", direction: "in", from: "4521", to: ["vivalence_office_bot"], at: "…", body: "did the notary invoice arrive?", external: { id: "918" } },
           CHAT: { platform: "telegram", chat: "4521", sender: { id: "4521", name: "beef" }, replyTo: null } },
  symbols: [{ slug: "message" }, { slug: "message.channel.telegram" }, { slug: "message.direction.in" }, { slug: "message.status.unread" }] }
#+end_src

*** service masks (the daemon's =consume=) + the service provider shape

#+begin_src javascript
consume: {
  mail:     { module: "@commons/service/mail",
              statics: { imap: () => new Url(paladin.env.get("VIVA_MAIL_IMAP")), smtp: () => new Url(paladin.env.get("VIVA_MAIL_SMTP")) },
              secrets: { user: () => paladin.secret.get("SECRET_VIVA_MAIL_USER"), password: () => paladin.secret.get("SECRET_VIVA_MAIL_PASSWORD") } },
  telegram: { module: "@commons/service/telegram", statics: {}, secrets: { token: () => paladin.secret.get("SECRET_VIVA_TELEGRAM_TOKEN") } },
}
// provider(mask) → { pull({ since }) → [message], send(message) → { external: { id } }, watch(handler) → stop }
// (commons/services/nlp/service.viva.js is the precedent: manifest + provider + optional tools)
#+end_src

*** freight — attachments

=modes/office/email= declares =new Freight("attachments")= under its minted mountpoint
(=<daemon mountpoint>/mode_office_email/attachments/<slug>/<name>=); =MAIL.attachments[].path=
is freight-relative; the freight url serves it to the App.

** entities

** files

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/domain/vledger/… · modes/ledger/… · instances/vcompany/ledger.js

* screens — the office in kajuit (his design import, worked out)

Source: claude.ai/design project =f7686382-998d-4fe0-9d86-28ddae58839a= ("VDex design system"),
files =vcompany Screens.dc.html= (00 shell · M1–M5 mobile · 01–11 apps, 170 kB) and =vcompany
System.dc.html= (zones · roots · type · drapes · kajuit chrome · §06 the office layer, 14 parts ·
§07 rules), read whole this session. Its =github.md= maps 00 to the EXISTING shell (=+layout= ·
=+page= · =geometry= · panels · bones · =Dock=) and 01–11 to "drapes + Dock vocabulary; content
from m53-vcompany.org (new)". The handoff says /"Implement"/; the house law says the patch lands
per item on go — every file below is verbatim against the tree as read, nothing under =systems/=
=subsystems/= or the package is touched.

** the map — screen → owner · file · tier

Symbolic. =owner= ∈ kajuit · drapes · package. =tier= = which waterfall the file rides. =status= ∈
EXISTS (in the tree, measured) · READY (file verbatim below) · PROSE (a delta, no hunk) · CALL.

| id    | screen                 | owner   | file                                        | tier | status  |
|-------+------------------------+---------+---------------------------------------------+------+---------|
| 00    | shell · desk open      | kajuit  | +page · panels a/b/c · bones · Dock         | —    | EXISTS  |
| M     | mobile · the rules     | kajuit  | geometry home · Dock 820 · coarse · safe    | —    | EXISTS  |
| M     | mobile · the apps      | package | every App below (=narrow= · sheet · 44px)   | —    | READY   |
| P1–P5 | office parts (§06)     | drapes  | Key · Tile · Plate · Entry · Empty · Pip+   | b2   | LANDED  |
| 01 02 | desk                   | package | officer/ceo/buffer/Desk.svelte              | T5   | READY   |
| 03 04 | vdex · reader · pdf    | package | office/vdex/buffer/Vdex.svelte              | T1   | LANDED  |
| 05 06 | mail · inbox · thread  | package | office/email/buffer/Inbox.svelte            | T2   | READY   |
| 07    | chat                   | package | office/chat/buffer/Chat.svelte              | T2   | READY   |
| 08    | officer dock           | kajuit  | panels/a/widgets/Dock.svelte (header)       | T5   | PROSE   |
| 09 10 | roster · offices       | package | domain/voffice/buffer/Voffice.svelte        | T0+  | READY   |
| 11    | calendar · agenda      | package | office/calendar/buffer/Agenda.svelte        | T2.c | RULED · a mode |

*** 00 — the shell
Nothing to build. The design re-draws the shell at the default pincer (33 % × 40 %, orientation 0)
with the desk open and the chat docked; every element is in the tree: =panels/a/a.svelte= hosts
=Frame= + the per-terminal =Dock=; =bones/{shoulder,pincer,crown,spine}=; =panels/b= sections
=bridge= · =lighthouse= · =terminals=; =panels/c= = d · twig · e · twig · f. The crown's tabs
(=desk= · =vdex= · =mail= · =ceo=) are terminals; an App opens as the terminal's buffer
(=terminal.buffer = …=, =Card.svelte='s precedent).

*** M — the rules, where each lives
MEASURED: pincer home ={x: 0, y: 1}= (=stores/bridge/bridge.js:68=) → at home the shoulder is
=pincer.x − HALF= = 0 wide and the spine 0 tall (=geometry.js bonesForOrientation=), panel a is
the whole phone above a 45 px crown — the design's M1. The Dock flips at 820 px
(=Dock.svelte:102= =narrow=), reads =(pointer: coarse)= (=:152=, =:1697=), safe areas come from
=client.html:23-36= (=--safe-area-*=), the type scale is the user's (=FONT_SIZES=). The apps
inherit ONE rule each, the Dock's: =bind:clientWidth= → =narrow = width < 820= → side pane above,
list › detail below (never both); rows ≥ 44 px, keys 44 px, chips 36 px under =(pointer: coarse)=;
hover-only actions always visible there. No container queries — the Dock's own measure.

*** P — the office parts (System §06), where each lives
Fourteen parts in the design; five are general and go to drapes (scoped-var family, the =Section=
· =Pip= · =Chip= aesthetic — NOT the Tailwind control kit, which is dead-in-live per the pincer
pass); the rest are ontology-shaped and stay in the mode that owns the ontology (no leaving the
mode; commons has zero cross-module Svelte imports — measured).

| part                        | lives in                          | new            |
|-----------------------------+-----------------------------------+----------------|
| Key (the outline action)    | drapes =controls/Key.svelte=      | yes            |
| Count tile                  | drapes =display/Tile.svelte=      | yes            |
| Officer plate               | drapes =display/Plate.svelte=     | yes            |
| Message body in / out       | drapes =display/Entry.svelte=     | yes            |
| Empty state (verb + trace)  | drapes =display/Empty.svelte=     | yes (Frame keeps its own — FORK: fold later) |
| Pip glow (unread)           | drapes =display/Pip.svelte=       | +1 prop        |
| Coordinate · Format         | vdex =buffer/Vdex.svelte=         | mode-local     |
| Document row · JD tree      | vdex =buffer/Vdex.svelte=         | mode-local     |
| Message row · Attachment    | email =buffer/Inbox.svelte=       | mode-local     |
| Harness section viewer      | kajuit =Dock.svelte= (=context=)  | UNMEASURED     |
| Brief · Boot line           | ceo =buffer/Desk.svelte=          | mode-local     |
| Event row · Day head        | calendar =buffer/Agenda.svelte=   | mode-local     |

*** the token map — design → dapper
=--sN/--cN/--bN= → =--colors-skeleton-N-{surface,contrast,boundary}= · =--primary= → =--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base=
(hover = =color-mix=) · =--warning/--success/--danger/--info= → =--colors-skeleton-0-<role>-base= · the design's
=--secondary= (indigo) → =info-base= (same root) · =--text-support= · =--text-body= · =--shadow-soft= exist as
is · =--font-heading= → =--font-family-sans-heading= · =--font-text= → =--font-family-sans-text= ·
=--font-code= → =--font-family-code=. Sizes, closest token: .55 → =2xs= · .6 → =xs= · .7–.85 → =sm= ·
.875 → =md= · .9–1 → =base= · 1.2–1.4 → =lg= · 1.6 → =xl= · 1.8 → =2xl= · 2 → =3xl=.

*** what an App can reach (measured)
Five props from =Frame= (=drapes/panels/Frame.svelte:60-66=): =terminal= · =daemon= · =mode= ·
=thread= · =buffer=. =daemon.entities.<type>.find(where, options)= (hello-world's App) · a mode's
own routes =mode.call.<route>(input)= (EXPOSED) · another mode's routes through its entity
(=(await daemon.entities.mode.findOne({ type, slug })).call.<route>=, the same trait) ·
=daemon.cortex.find({ type, via })= (the Dock reads it) · =terminal.buffer = <buffer>= (Card.svelte).
GATE ITEMS (unmeasured): =find= options =orderBy= pass through the remote repository · a buffer
minted server-side (=/open= below) merges into =daemon.entities.buffer= before =terminal.buffer=
takes it · the client =Mode= carries =statics= (=Voffice.svelte= reads =mode.statics= for the
mountpoint and formats — falls back to =metadata=).

** files — drapes (b2) — LANDED on /"go"/

The five blocks below landed verbatim; the two barrel diffs were stale against the tree (display already exported =Markdown= · =Org= · =Pdf=; controls carries =Link= · =Keyboard= · =ViewportLock= · =persist= · =visible= · =drag=) and were written against the tree as read. Gate: =subsystems/drapes/tests/parts.test.js= compiles the six parts as runes components with zero warnings and asserts the barrels export them (=svelte/compiler= resolves through the root config).

*** subsystems/drapes/controls/Key.svelte
new. The outline action — every "review reply" · "open 22.04" · "edit" · "↗ ask" in the design is
one of these. Tones: =primary= (bordered primary at 45 %), =muted= (boundary), =danger=. 44 px tall
under a coarse pointer.

#+begin_src svelte
<script>
  let { label, tone = "muted", active = false, disabled = false, title = null, onclick, children } = $props();
</script>

<button class="key {tone}" class:active {disabled} {title} onclick={disabled ? undefined : onclick}>
  {#if children}{@render children()}{:else}{label}{/if}
</button>

<style>
  .key {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 7px 14px;
    border: 1px solid var(--colors-skeleton-3-boundary);
    border-radius: 2px;
    background: transparent;
    color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--colors-skeleton-3-contrast) 70%, transparent);
    font: inherit;
    font-family: var(--font-family-code);
    font-size: var(--font-size-2xs);
    letter-spacing: 0.14em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: nowrap;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: color 0.12s, border-color 0.12s, background 0.12s;
  }
  .key:hover {
    border-color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
  }
  .key.primary {
    border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base) 45%, transparent);
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
  }
  .key.primary:hover,
  .key.active {
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base) 9%, transparent);
    border-color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
  }
  .key.danger {
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-danger-base);
  }
  .key.danger:hover {
    border-color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-danger-base);
  }
  .key:disabled {
    opacity: 0.35;
    cursor: default;
  }
  @media (pointer: coarse) {
    .key {
      min-height: 44px;
      padding: 0 14px;
    }
  }
</style>
#+end_src

*** subsystems/drapes/display/Plate.svelte

#+begin_src svelte
<script>
  import Pip from "./Pip.svelte";

  let { mark, name, description = null, state = null, size = "md", muted = false, children } = $props();
  const marks = { sm: "26px", md: "34px", lg: "38px" };
</script>

<div class="plate" class:muted>
  <span class="plate-mark" style:width={marks[size]} style:height={marks[size]}>{mark}</span>
  <div class="plate-text">
    <span class="plate-name">{name}</span>
    {#if description}<span class="plate-description">{description}</span>{/if}
    {#if children}{@render children()}{/if}
  </div>
  {#if state}
    <span class="plate-state {state.tone ?? 'muted'}"><Pip size={6} tone={state.tone ?? "muted"} glow={state.tone === "primary"} />{state.label}</span>
  {/if}
</div>

<style>
  .plate {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 12px;
    min-width: 0;
    font-family: var(--font-family-code);
  }
  .plate-mark {
    display: grid;
    place-items: center;
    flex: none;
    border: 1px solid var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    letter-spacing: 0.1em;
    font-weight: 600;
  }
  .plate.muted .plate-mark {
    border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--colors-skeleton-3-contrast) 40%, transparent);
    color: var(--text-body);
  }
  .plate-text {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 3px;
    min-width: 0;
    flex: 1;
  }
  .plate-name {
    font-family: var(--font-family-sans-heading);
    font-weight: 500;
    font-size: var(--font-size-md);
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
  .plate-description {
    font-size: var(--font-size-xs);
    color: var(--text-support);
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
  }
  .plate-state {
    display: inline-flex;
    align-items: center;
    gap: 6px;
    flex: none;
    font-size: var(--font-size-2xs);
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    color: var(--text-support);
  }
  .plate-state.primary {
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-primary-base);
  }
</style>

** files — the apps (package, per tier)

Every App: five props, =bind:clientWidth= → =narrow=, one measure, the Dock's own rules; every
number from a query, nothing hardcoded that the design mocked; every action a route on its own mode
or a jump through another mode's entity. Dates through =Intl= in the App — the belt's =time.since=
is kajuit's (=gestalten/belt/time.js= sits in kajuit's typology, not the package's reach).

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/vdex/buffer/Vdex.svelte — LANDED (the block below IS the landed file)
The READY draft was written before the rows snapshot existed and had two defects against it, found by reading
=vdex-rows.snapshot.json=: a row carries =symbol.vdex = { area: "20-29", category: "22", id: { "22": "04" } }= —
=id= is an OBJECT keyed by category, not a string, so the draft's =under= never matched an id node (counts 0, no
leaves); and category visibility was keyed on the category itself (=expanded["12"]=), not on its area, so no
category ever appeared. Landed instead: =place(literal)= · =under(literal, node)= by depth · =leaves(node)= (an
expanded category lists its loose files, an id its documents) · =coordinateOf= for the head chip (the draft read
=slug.split(".").slice(0, 2)= → =12.notes= for a loose file) · =areaOf= · =rank=/=before= (structural sort — a
string sort put category =20= before its area =20-29=) · =shown(node, areas)= (area › category › id, each behind its
ancestors). The unused =Chip= import and =boot= state dropped. Gate: =tests/app.test.js= compiles it with zero
warnings; =paladin.bundler= bundles it through the drapes barrel (2.73 MB, the stub 2.59).
REPLACES T1's stub (c5). 03 · 04 · M2: the JD tree (area › category › id, minted symbols, counts
from the documents) with a find box; the reader head (coordinate chip · title · slug · path ·
format · readable · size · age); read / edit; the body through =Markdown= · =Org= · =Json= ·
=Paragraph= or =Pdf= over the cargo url with the =binary= line under it; edit = a scoped
=<textarea>= (beef: /"textarea."/ — the Tailwind =Textarea= is the dead-in-live kit) → =/write=.
Under 820 px the tree is a sheet over the reader (M2), the coordinate key opens it, 44 px rows.

#+begin_src svelte
<script>
  import { Section, Pip, Key, Empty, Markdown, Org, Json, Paragraph, Pdf } from "@vivalence/drapes";

  let { terminal, daemon, mode, thread, buffer } = $props();

  let width = $state(0);
  const narrow = $derived(width > 0 && width < 820);

  let symbols = $state([]);
  let documents = $state([]);
  let open = $state(null);
  let text = $state("");
  let editing = $state(false);
  let query = $state("");
  let expanded = $state({});
  let sheet = $state(false);

  const COORDINATE = /^vdex\.(area|category|id)\./;
  const KIND = { area: 0, category: 1, id: 2 };
  const coordinate = (symbol) => symbol.slug.split(".").slice(2).join(".");
  const kind = (symbol) => symbol.slug.split(".")[1];
  const named = (symbol) => symbol.trait?.LABELED?.name ?? "";
  // a row carries symbol.vdex = { area: "20-29", category: "22", id: { "22": "04" } }; its symbols are not populated over the wire
  const place = (literal) => literal.symbol?.vdex ?? {};
  const under = (literal, node) => {
    const { area, category, id } = place(literal);
    if (node.depth === 0) return area === node.coordinate;
    if (node.depth === 1) return category === node.coordinate;
    const [head, tail] = node.coordinate.split(".");
    return id?.[head] === tail;
  };
  const leaves = (node) =>
    documents.filter((literal) => under(literal, node) && (node.depth === 2 || (!place(literal).id && (node.depth === 1 || !place(literal).category))));
  const coordinateOf = (literal) => {
    const { area, category, id } = place(literal);
    return id ? Object.entries(id)[0].join(".") : (category ?? area ?? null);
  };
  const areaOf = (category, areas) =>
    areas.find((area) => {
      const [low, high] = area.split("-").map(Number);
      return Number(category) >= low && Number(category) <= high;
    });
  const rank = (node) => {
    const [head, tail] = node.coordinate.split(".");
    if (node.depth === 0) return [Number(head.split("-")[0]), 0, 0, 0];
    return [Math.floor(Number(head) / 10) * 10, Number(head), Number(tail ?? 0), node.depth];
  };
  const before = (a, b) => {
    const [x, y] = [rank(a), rank(b)];
    return x[0] - y[0] || x[1] - y[1] || x[2] - y[2] || x[3] - y[3];
  };
  const shown = (node, areas) =>
    node.depth === 0 ||
    (node.depth === 1 && !!expanded[areaOf(node.coordinate, areas)]) ||
    (node.depth === 2 && !!expanded[areaOf(node.coordinate.split(".")[0], areas)] && !!expanded[node.coordinate.split(".")[0]]);
  const format = (literal) => literal.trait.DOCUMENT.format;
  const size = (bytes) => (bytes < 1024 ? `${bytes} B` : bytes < 1048576 ? `${Math.round(bytes / 1024)} kB` : `${(bytes / 1048576).toFixed(1)} MB`);
  const age = (iso) => {
    const days = Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso)) / 86400000));
    return days < 1 ? "today" : days < 7 ? `${days}d` : days < 30 ? `${Math.round(days / 7)}w` : days < 365 ? `${Math.round(days / 30)}mo` : `${Math.round(days / 365)}y`;
  };

  const tree = $derived.by(() => {
    const filter = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    const nodes = symbols
      .filter((symbol) => COORDINATE.test(symbol.slug))
      .map((symbol) => ({ symbol, depth: KIND[kind(symbol)], coordinate: coordinate(symbol), name: named(symbol) }))
      .map((node) => ({ ...node, count: documents.filter((literal) => under(literal, node)).length }))
      .sort(before);
    if (filter) return nodes.filter((node) => `${node.coordinate} ${node.name}`.toLowerCase().includes(filter));
    const areas = nodes.filter((node) => node.depth === 0).map((node) => node.coordinate);
    return nodes.filter((node) => shown(node, areas));
  });
  const found = $derived.by(() => {
    const filter = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!filter) return null;
    return documents.filter((literal) => `${literal.slug} ${literal.trait.DOCUMENT.title}`.toLowerCase().includes(filter));
  });
  const unfiled = $derived(documents.filter((literal) => !place(literal).area).length);

  async function load() {
    symbols = await mode.call.tree({});
    documents = await mode.call.documents({ limit: 2000 });
    if (buffer?.data?.open) await read(buffer.data.open);
    else if (buffer?.data?.id) query = buffer.data.id;
  }
  async function read(slug) {
    open = await mode.call.document({ slug });
    text = open?.text ?? "";
    editing = false;
    sheet = false;
  }
  async function save() {
    open = await mode.call.write({ slug: open.literal.slug, text });
    editing = false;
    documents = await mode.call.documents({ limit: 2000 });
  }
  const toggle = (node) => (expanded = { ...expanded, [node.coordinate]: !expanded[node.coordinate] });
  load();
</script>

<div class="vdex" class:narrow bind:clientWidth={width}>
  <aside class="tree" class:sheet={narrow} class:open={!narrow || sheet}>
    {#if narrow}<div class="grip"><span></span></div>{/if}
    <div class="tree-head">
      <Section label="jdex" count={documents.length} />
      <label class="find"><span class="find-glyph">⌕</span><input placeholder="find · coordinate, title, slug" bind:value={query} /></label>
    </div>
    <div class="nodes">
      {#if found}
        {#each found as literal (literal.slug)}
          <button class="node leaf" class:on={open?.literal.slug === literal.slug} onclick={() => read(literal.slug)}>
            <Pip size={4} tone={format(literal) === "pdf" ? "warning" : literal.trait.DOCUMENT.readable ? "primary" : "muted"} />
            <span class="node-label">{literal.slug}</span>
            <span class="node-meta">{format(literal)}</span>
          </button>
        {/each}
      {:else}
        {#each tree as node (node.symbol.slug)}
          <button class="node" class:area={node.depth === 0} style:padding-left="{2 + node.depth * 22}px" onclick={() => toggle(node)}>
            <span class="caret">{node.depth < 2 ? (expanded[node.coordinate] ? "▾" : "▸") : ""}</span>
            <Pip size={node.depth === 0 ? 6 : 4} tone={expanded[node.coordinate] && node.depth === 0 ? "primary" : "muted"} />
            <span class="node-label">{node.coordinate} {node.name}</span>
            <span class="node-meta">{node.count}</span>
          </button>
          {#if expanded[node.coordinate]}
            {#each leaves(node) as literal (literal.slug)}
              <button class="node leaf" class:on={open?.literal.slug === literal.slug} style:padding-left="{2 + (node.depth + 1) * 22}px" onclick={() => read(literal.slug)}>
                <Pip size={4} tone={format(literal) === "pdf" ? "warning" : literal.trait.DOCUMENT.readable ? "primary" : "muted"} />
                <span class="node-label">{literal.slug}</span>
                <span class="node-meta">{format(literal)}</span>
              </button>
            {/each}
          {/if}
        {/each}
        {#if unfiled}<div class="node unfiled"><span class="node-label">unfiled</span><span class="node-meta">{unfiled}</span></div>{/if}
      {/if}
      {#if !documents.length}
        <Empty verb="empty jdex" trace="0 documents · drop files in the mountpoint and reboot" />
      {/if}
    </div>
    <div class="boot"><span class="tag">[VDEX]</span> {documents.length} · {symbols.filter((symbol) => COORDINATE.test(symbol.slug)).length}</div>
  </aside>

  <section class="reader">
    {#if open}
      {@const { title, format: kind, readable, bytes, mtime, asset } = open.literal.trait.DOCUMENT}
      <header class="head">
        {#if narrow}<Key label="‹ {place(open.literal).category ?? 'jdex'}" onclick={() => (sheet = true)} />{/if}
        <div class="head-text">
          <div class="head-title">
            <span class="coordinate" class:filled={!!place(open.literal).id}>{coordinateOf(open.literal) ?? "—"}</span>
            <span class="title">{title}</span>
          </div>
          <span class="head-line">{open.literal.slug} · {asset.path}</span>
        </div>
        {#if !narrow}
          <span class="head-meta"><Pip size={4} tone={kind === "pdf" ? "warning" : "primary"} />{kind} · {readable ? "readable" : "indexed"} · {size(bytes)} · {age(mtime)}</span>
        {/if}
        {#if readable && kind !== "pdf"}
          <div class="segment">
            <button class:on={!editing} onclick={() => (editing = false)}>read</button>
            <button class:on={editing} onclick={() => (editing = true)}>edit</button>
          </div>
        {/if}
        {#if kind === "pdf"}<Key label="↓ cargo" onclick={() => open.url && window.open(open.url, "_blank")} disabled={!open.url} />{/if}
        <Key label="↗ ask" disabled title="the officer stage" />
      </header>
      <div class="body">
        {#if !readable}
          <Empty verb="not readable" trace="{kind} — indexed only; open it where it lives: {asset.path}" />
        {:else if kind === "pdf"}
          <div class="pdf"><Pdf url={open.url} /></div>
          <div class="binary"><span class="warn">binary</span><span class="rule"></span><span>vdex_fs_read refuses this format — tools get the cargo url and "open it in the app"; an ocr service comes later.</span></div>
        {:else if editing}
          <textarea class="editor" bind:value={text} spellcheck="false"></textarea>
          <div class="tray"><Key label="save" tone="primary" onclick={save} /><Key label="discard" tone="danger" onclick={() => ((text = open.text ?? ""), (editing = false))} /></div>
        {:else}
          <div class="prose">
            {#if kind === "md" || kind === "mdx"}<Markdown {text} />
            {:else if kind === "org"}<Org {text} />
            {:else if kind === "json" || kind === "jsonc"}<Json value={JSON.parse(text || "null")} />
            {:else}<Paragraph>{text}</Paragraph>{/if}
          </div>
        {/if}
      </div>
    {:else}
      <Empty verb="nothing open" trace="pick a document — a coordinate, a title, a slug" />
    {/if}
  </section>
</div>

<style>
  .vdex {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    height: 100%;
    min-height: 0;
    overflow: hidden;
    background: var(--colors-skeleton-0-surface);
    color: var(--colors-skeleton-0-contrast);
    font-family: var(--font-family-code);
  }
  .tree {
    flex: 0 0 270px;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;

    overflow: hidden;

    letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  }
  .tree.sheet {
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    right: 0;
    bottom: 0;

    z-index: 2;
  }
  .tree.sheet.open {

  }

    padding: 8px 0 4px;

    width: 36px;
    height: 4px;

    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--colors-skeleton-2-contrast) 25%, transparent);
  }

    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;

  }
  .find {

    align-items: center;

    padding: 0 8px;
    border: 1px solid var(--colors-skeleton-2-boundary);

    background: var(--colors-skeleton-1-surface);
    font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
    color: var(--text-support);
  }

The T2 heading exists as prose; the App fixes the routes it calls. Handlers =(input, ctx)=.

#+begin_src javascript
import { Aperture } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { pull, send } from "./sync.js";

const mail = (ctx, where = {}, options = {}) =>
  ctx.daemon.entities.literal.find({ ontology: "message", symbols: [{ slug: "message.channel.email" }], ...where }, { orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" }, ...options });

export const aperture = new Aperture()
  .open("/inbox", (input, ctx) => mail(ctx, {}, { limit: input.limit ?? 200 }))
  .open("/message", (input, ctx) => ctx.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ ontology: "message", slug: input.slug }))
  .open("/read", async (input, ctx) => {
    const held = await ctx.daemon.entities.literal.findOne({ ontology: "message", slug: input.slug });
    held.symbols.remove((symbol) => symbol.slug === "message.status.unread");
    held.symbols.add(await ctx.daemon.entities.symbol.findOne({ slug: "message.status.read" }));
    await ctx.daemon.entities.em.flush();
    return held;
  })
  .open("/send", (input, ctx) => send(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, input));   // { to, subject, body, inReplyTo? } → the out row, parent flips to answered
#+end_src

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/email/buffer/Inbox.svelte
05 · 06 · M3: the list (filters unread · all · out · ◈) › the preview (subject · from/to/id grid ·
direction/status · keys reply · mark read · consult cfo) with the thread folded from =uses= and
=MAIL.inReplyTo= client-side (no populate), attachments as chips over the freight url, and the
reply composer → =/send=. Under 820 px: list only, tap → thread, ‹ back.

#+begin_src svelte
<script>
  import { Section, Pip, Chip, Key, Entry, Empty } from "@vivalence/drapes";

  let { terminal, daemon, mode, thread, buffer } = $props();

  let width = $state(0);
  const narrow = $derived(width > 0 && width < 820);

  let rows = $state([]);
  let filter = $state("unread");
  let open = $state(null);
  let composing = $state(false);
  let draft = $state("");

  const has = (row, slug) => row.symbols?.some?.((symbol) => symbol.slug === slug) ?? false;
  const unread = (row) => has(row, "message.status.unread") || row.symbol?.message?.status === "unread";
  const answered = (row) => has(row, "message.status.answered") || row.symbol?.message?.status === "answered";
  const out = (row) => row.trait.MESSAGE.direction === "out";
  const attachments = (row) => row.trait.MAIL?.attachments ?? [];
  const when = (iso) => {
    const at = new Date(iso);
    const days = Math.round((Date.now() - at) / 86400000);
    if (days < 1) return at.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
    if (days < 7) return at.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: "short" }).toLowerCase();
    return at.toLocaleDateString([], { month: "short", day: "numeric" }).toLowerCase();
  };
  const preview = (row) => row.trait.MESSAGE.body.replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 90);
  const address = mode.statics?.address ?? mode.metadata?.address ?? "office";

  const shown = $derived(
    rows.filter((row) => (filter === "unread" ? unread(row) : filter === "out" ? out(row) : filter === "attachments" ? attachments(row).length : true)),
  );
  const conversation = $derived.by(() => {
    if (!open) return [];
    const id = open.trait.MAIL.messageId;
    const parents = new Set([open.trait.MAIL.inReplyTo, ...(open.trait.MAIL.references ?? [])].filter(Boolean));
    return rows
      .filter((row) => row.slug === open.slug || row.trait.MAIL.inReplyTo === id || parents.has(row.trait.MAIL.messageId) || row.uses?.some?.((used) => used.slug === open.slug))
      .sort((a, b) => (a.trait.MESSAGE.at < b.trait.MESSAGE.at ? -1 : 1));
  });
  const unreadCount = $derived(rows.filter(unread).length);

  async function load() {
    rows = await mode.call.inbox({ limit: 200 });
    if (buffer?.data?.open) open = rows.find((row) => row.slug === buffer.data.open) ?? (await mode.call.message({ slug: buffer.data.open }));
  }
  async function show(row) {
    open = row;
    composing = false;
    if (unread(row) && !out(row)) {
      await mode.call.read({ slug: row.slug });
      rows = await mode.call.inbox({ limit: 200 });
      open = rows.find((held) => held.slug === row.slug) ?? row;
    }
  }
  async function send() {
    if (!draft.trim()) return;
    await mode.call.send({ to: open.trait.MESSAGE.from, subject: `Re: ${open.trait.MAIL.subject.replace(/^re:\s*/i, "")}`, body: draft, inReplyTo: open.trait.MAIL.messageId });
    draft = "";
    composing = false;
    rows = await mode.call.inbox({ limit: 200 });
  }
  async function file(row, attachment) {
    const vdex = await daemon.entities.mode.findOne({ type: "office", slug: "vdex" });
    if (!vdex) return;
    terminal.buffer = await vdex.call.open({ slug: attachment.filed, thread: thread?.id });
  }
  load();
</script>

<div class="mail" class:narrow bind:clientWidth={width}>
  {#if !narrow || !open}
    <aside class="list">
      <div class="list-head">
        <Section label={address} count="{rows.length} · {unreadCount} unread" />
        <div class="chips">
          <Chip label="unread" active={filter === "unread"} onclick={() => (filter = "unread")} />
          <Chip label="all" active={filter === "all"} onclick={() => (filter = "all")} />
          <Chip label="out" active={filter === "out"} onclick={() => (filter = "out")} />
          <Chip label="◈ attachments" active={filter === "attachments"} onclick={() => (filter = "attachments")} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="rows">
        {#each shown as row (row.slug)}
          <button class="row" class:on={open?.slug === row.slug} class:dim={!unread(row) && open?.slug !== row.slug} onclick={() => show(row)}>
            <Pip size={6} tone={unread(row) ? "primary" : "muted"} glow={unread(row)} />
            <span class="glyph">{out(row) ? "↑" : "@"}</span>
            <div class="row-text">
              <div class="row-from"><span class="from" class:primary={open?.slug === row.slug}>{out(row) ? row.trait.MESSAGE.to[0] : row.trait.MESSAGE.from}</span>
                {#if attachments(row).length}<span class="tag">◈ {attachments(row).length}</span>{/if}
                {#if answered(row)}<span class="tag ok">↩ answered</span>{/if}
                {#if out(row)}<span class="tag">↑ out</span>{/if}</div>
              <span class="subject">{row.trait.MAIL.subject}</span>
              <span class="preview">{preview(row)}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="time">{when(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span>
          </button>
        {/each}
        {#if !rows.length}<Empty verb="no mailbox" trace="VIVA_MAIL_IMAP · instance/doctor" />{/if}
        {#if rows.length && !shown.length}<Empty verb="nothing here" trace="{filter}" />{/if}
      </div>
      <div class="boot"><span class="tag">[MAIL]</span> {address} · {rows.length} messages · {unreadCount} unread</div>
    </aside>
  {/if}

  {#if !narrow || open}
    <section class="view">
      {#if open}
        <header class="head">
          {#if narrow}<Key label="‹" onclick={() => (open = null)} />{/if}
          <div class="head-text">
            <span class="subject-big">{open.trait.MAIL.subject}</span>
            <div class="grid">
              <span class="k">from</span><span>{open.trait.MESSAGE.from}</span>
              <span class="k">to</span><span>{open.trait.MESSAGE.to.join(", ")}</span>
              <span class="k">id</span><span class="k">{open.slug} · {open.trait.MAIL.messageId}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="head-side">
            <span class="state">{out(open) ? "↑ out" : "↓ in"} · email · {when(open.trait.MESSAGE.at)} · {unread(open) ? "unread" : answered(open) ? "answered" : "read"}</span>
            <div class="keys">
              <Key label="reply" tone="primary" onclick={() => (composing = true)} />
              {#if unread(open)}<Key label="mark read" onclick={() => show(open)} />{/if}
              <Key label="consult cfo" disabled title="the officer stage" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </header>
        <div class="thread">
          {#each conversation as row (row.slug)}
            <Entry side={out(row) ? "out" : "in"}>
              {#snippet head()}
                {#if out(row)}<span>{when(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span><span>·</span><span class="you">{row.trait.MESSAGE.from}</span><span class="arrow">↑</span>
                {:else}<span class="arrow in">↓</span><span>{row.trait.MESSAGE.from}</span><span>{when(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span><span>{row.slug}</span>{#if attachments(row).length}<span>◈ {attachments(row).length}</span>{/if}{/if}
              {/snippet}
              {row.trait.MESSAGE.body}
              {#snippet foot()}
                {#each attachments(row) as attachment (attachment.path)}
                  <span class="attachment"><span class="tag">◈</span>{attachment.path.split("/").pop()}<span class="dim">{attachment.type} · {Math.round(attachment.bytes / 1024)} kB</span>
                    {#if attachment.filed}<span class="rule"></span><button class="link" onclick={() => file(row, attachment)}>filed → {attachment.filed}</button>{/if}</span>
                {/each}
                {#if row.uses?.length}<span>uses → {row.uses.map((used) => used.slug).join(" · ")}</span>{/if}
              {/snippet}
            </Entry>
          {/each}
          {#if composing}
            <Entry side="out" draft>
              {#snippet head()}<span class="warn">draft</span><span>·</span><span class="you">{address} → {open.trait.MESSAGE.from}</span><span class="arrow">↑</span>{/snippet}
              <textarea class="composer" bind:value={draft} placeholder="reply as {address}…" rows="5"></textarea>
              {#snippet foot()}
                <span>uses → {open.slug} · in-reply-to set</span><span class="rule"></span>
                <Key label="email_send" tone="primary" onclick={send} disabled={!draft.trim()} />
                <Key label="discard" tone="danger" onclick={() => ((draft = ""), (composing = false))} />
              {/snippet}
            </Entry>
          {/if}
        </div>
      {:else}
        <Empty verb="nothing open" trace="pick a message" />
      {/if}
    </section>
  {/if}
</div>

#+begin_src javascript
import { Aperture } from "@vivalence/typology";
import { send } from "./sync.js";

export const aperture = new Aperture()
  .open("/messages", (input, ctx) =>
    ctx.daemon.entities.literal.find(
      { ontology: "message", symbols: [{ slug: `message.channel.${input.platform ?? "telegram"}` }] },
      { orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" }, limit: input.limit ?? 500 },
    ),
  )
  .open("/send", (input, ctx) => send(ctx.daemon, ctx.mode, input));   // { platform, chat, body, replyTo? } → the out row
#+end_src

*** ~/.viva/registry/vcompany/modes/office/chat/buffer/Chat.svelte
07: chats (grouped by =CHAT.chat=, platform chips from =statics.platforms=) › the conversation
(entries in/out, =uses → replyTo=) › the composer → =/send=. Under 820 px: list › conversation.

#+begin_src svelte
<script>
  import { Section, Pip, Chip, Key, Entry, Empty } from "@vivalence/drapes";

  let { terminal, daemon, mode, thread, buffer } = $props();

  let width = $state(0);
  const narrow = $derived(width > 0 && width < 820);

  const platforms = mode.statics?.platforms ?? mode.metadata?.platforms ?? ["telegram"];
  const GLYPH = { telegram: "▲", slack: "▣", email: "@" };

  let platform = $state(platforms[0]);
  let rows = $state([]);
  let chat = $state(null);
  let draft = $state("");

  const out = (row) => row.trait.MESSAGE.direction === "out";
  const unread = (row) => row.symbols?.some?.((symbol) => symbol.slug === "message.status.unread") || row.symbol?.message?.status === "unread";
  const time = (iso) => new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
  const day = (iso) => {
    const days = Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso)) / 86400000);
    return days < 1 ? "today" : days < 7 ? new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: "short" }).toLowerCase() : new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString([], { month: "short", day: "numeric" }).toLowerCase();
  };

  const chats = $derived.by(() => {
    const byChat = new Map();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const key = row.trait.CHAT.chat;
      const held = byChat.get(key) ?? { key, name: row.trait.CHAT.sender?.name ?? key, last: row, unread: 0 };
      if (row.trait.MESSAGE.at > held.last.trait.MESSAGE.at) held.last = row;
      if (!out(row)) held.name = row.trait.CHAT.sender?.name ?? held.name;
      if (unread(row)) held.unread += 1;
      byChat.set(key, held);
    }
    return [...byChat.values()].sort((a, b) => (a.last.trait.MESSAGE.at < b.last.trait.MESSAGE.at ? 1 : -1));
  });
  const conversation = $derived(rows.filter((row) => row.trait.CHAT.chat === chat).sort((a, b) => (a.trait.MESSAGE.at < b.trait.MESSAGE.at ? -1 : 1)));
  const unreadCount = $derived(rows.filter(unread).length);

  async function load() {
    rows = await mode.call.messages({ platform, limit: 500 });
    if (!chat && buffer?.data?.chat) chat = buffer.data.chat;
  }
  async function send() {
    if (!draft.trim() || !chat) return;
    const last = conversation.at(-1);
    await mode.call.send({ platform, chat, body: draft, replyTo: last && !out(last) ? last.trait.MESSAGE.external.id : null });
    draft = "";
    await load();
  }
  $effect(() => {
    platform;
    load();
  });
</script>

<div class="chat" class:narrow bind:clientWidth={width}>
  {#if !narrow || !chat}
    <aside class="list">
      <div class="list-head">
        <Section label="chats" count={chats.length} />
        <div class="chips">
          {#each platforms as name (name)}
            <Chip label="{GLYPH[name] ?? '·'} {name}" active={platform === name} onclick={() => ((platform = name), (chat = null))} />
          {/each}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="rows">
        {#each chats as held (held.key)}
          <button class="row" class:on={chat === held.key} onclick={() => (chat = held.key)}>
            <span class="tick" class:on={chat === held.key}></span>
            <div class="row-text">
              <div class="row-from"><span class="from" class:primary={chat === held.key}>{held.name}</span><span class="tag">{platform}.{held.key}</span></div>
              <span class="preview">{held.last.trait.MESSAGE.body}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="time">{day(held.last.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span>
            {#if held.unread}<Pip size={6} tone="primary" glow />{/if}
          </button>
        {/each}
        {#if !rows.length}<Empty verb="no chats" trace="SECRET_VIVA_TELEGRAM_TOKEN · instance/doctor" />{/if}
      </div>
      <div class="boot"><span class="tag primary">[CHAT]</span> {platform} · {chats.length} chats · {unreadCount} unread</div>
    </aside>
  {/if}

  {#if !narrow || chat}
    <section class="view">
      {#if chat}
        {@const held = chats.find((candidate) => candidate.key === chat)}
        <header class="head">
          {#if narrow}<Key label="‹" onclick={() => (chat = null)} />{/if}
          <span class="glyph">{GLYPH[platform] ?? "·"}</span>
          <span class="who">{held?.name ?? chat} · {platform}.{chat}</span>
          <span class="spacer"></span>
          <Key label="↗ ask ceo" disabled title="the officer stage" />
        </header>
        <div class="thread">
          {#each conversation as row, index (row.slug)}
            {#if index === 0 || day(row.trait.MESSAGE.at) !== day(conversation[index - 1].trait.MESSAGE.at)}
              <div class="dayhead"><span></span><span class="dayhead-label">{day(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span><span></span></div>
            {/if}
            <Entry side={out(row) ? "out" : "in"}>
              {#snippet head()}
                {#if out(row)}<span>{time(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span><span>·</span><span class="you">office{row.trait.CHAT.via ? ` · via ${row.trait.CHAT.via}` : ""}</span><span class="arrow">↑</span>
                {:else}<span class="arrow in">↓</span><span>{row.trait.CHAT.sender?.name ?? row.trait.MESSAGE.from}</span><span>{time(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span><span>{row.slug}</span>{/if}
              {/snippet}
              {row.trait.MESSAGE.body}
              {#snippet foot()}
                {#if row.trait.CHAT.replyTo}<span>uses → {platform}.{chat}.{row.trait.CHAT.replyTo} · replyTo {row.trait.CHAT.replyTo}</span>{/if}
              {/snippet}
            </Entry>
          {/each}
        </div>
        <div class="composer">
          <textarea bind:value={draft} placeholder="reply as office…" rows="1" onkeydown={(event) => event.key === "Enter" && !event.shiftKey && (event.preventDefault(), send())}></textarea>
          <button class="send" class:armed={draft.trim()} onclick={send} aria-label="send">↵</button>
        </div>
      {:else}
        <Empty verb="no chat open" trace="pick one on the left" />
      {/if}
    </section>
  {/if}
</div>

01 · 02 · M1. Left: the plate (mark · name · company line from =daemon.statics.company= → the

#+begin_src svelte
<script>
  import { Section, Pip, Key, Tile, Plate, Empty } from "@vivalence/drapes";

  let { terminal, daemon, mode, thread, buffer } = $props();

  let width = $state(0);
  const narrow = $derived(width > 0 && width < 820);

  let inbox = $state([]);
  let documents = $state([]);
  let officers = $state([]);
  let offices = $state({});
  let brief = $state(null);

  const company = daemon.metadata?.company ?? daemon.statics?.company ?? {};
  const keyed = (daemon.cortex?.find?.({ type: "verbatim" })?.length ?? 0) > 0;
  const unread = (row) => row.symbols?.some?.((symbol) => symbol.slug === "message.status.unread") || row.symbol?.message?.status === "unread";
  const glyph = (row) => ({ email: "@", telegram: "▲", slack: "▣" })[row.trait.MESSAGE.channel] ?? "·";
  const who = (row) => row.trait.CHAT?.sender?.name ?? row.trait.MESSAGE.from;
  const subject = (row) => row.trait.MAIL?.subject ?? row.trait.MESSAGE.body;
  const when = (iso) => {
    const at = new Date(iso);
    const days = Math.round((Date.now() - at) / 86400000);
    return days < 1 ? at.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" }) : days < 7 ? at.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: "short" }).toLowerCase() : at.toLocaleDateString([], { month: "short", day: "numeric" }).toLowerCase();
  };
  const age = (iso) => {
    const days = Math.max(0, Math.round((Date.now() - new Date(iso)) / 86400000));
    return days < 1 ? "today" : days < 7 ? `${days}d` : days < 30 ? `${Math.round(days / 7)}w` : `${Math.round(days / 30)}mo`;
  };
  const now = new Date().toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: "short", month: "short", day: "numeric" }).toLowerCase() + " · " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });

  const unreadRows = $derived(inbox.filter(unread));
  const coordinates = $derived(new Set(documents.map((row) => row.symbol?.vdex?.id && Object.values(row.symbol.vdex.id)[0] && `${row.symbol.vdex.category}.${Object.values(row.symbol.vdex.id)[0]}`).filter(Boolean)).size);

  async function load() {
    const modes = await daemon.entities.mode.find();
    offices = Object.fromEntries(modes.filter((held) => held.type === "office").map((held) => [held.slug, held]));
    officers = modes.filter((held) => held.type === "officer");
    inbox = await daemon.entities.literal.find({ ontology: "message" }, { orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" }, limit: 50 });
    documents = await daemon.entities.literal.find({ ontology: "vdex" }, { orderBy: { updatedAt: "desc" }, limit: 500 });
    brief = mode.call.brief ? await mode.call.brief({}).catch(() => null) : null;
  }
  async function open(slug, data) {
    const owner = offices[slug];
    if (!owner?.call?.open) return;
    terminal.buffer = await owner.call.open({ ...data, thread: thread?.id });
  }
  load();
</script>

<div class="desk" class:narrow bind:clientWidth={width}>
  <div class="columns">
    <div class="left">
      <Plate mark={mode.slug.toUpperCase()} name={mode.name} description="{mode.description}{company.name ? ` · ${company.name}` : ''}{company.seat ? ` · ${company.seat}` : ''}{company.stage ? ` · ${company.stage}` : ''}" muted={!keyed}>
        <span class="clock">{now}</span>
      </Plate>
      {#if brief}
        <h2 class="lead">{brief.lead}</h2>
        <div class="prose">{#each brief.paragraphs as paragraph}<p>{paragraph}</p>{/each}</div>
        <div class="keys">
          {#each brief.actions ?? [] as action}
            <Key label={action.label} tone={action.primary ? "primary" : "muted"} onclick={() => action.open && open(action.open.mode, action.open)} />
          {/each}
        </div>
      {:else if !documents.length && !inbox.length}
        <h2 class="lead">The office is open. It is empty.</h2>
        <div class="prose">
          <p>The JDex holds nothing yet — drop the company's folders into the mountpoint and reboot; the boot indexes what it finds and mints the coordinates.</p>
          <p>No mailbox and no chat are connected. {keyed ? "" : "Without a key the officers answer deterministically, as the bot."}</p>
        </div>
      {:else}
        <h2 class="lead">{unreadRows.length ? `${unreadRows.length} unread.` : "Nothing unread."} {documents.length} documents on file.</h2>
        <div class="prose"><p>The morning brief arrives with the officer stage; until then the office speaks in numbers.</p></div>
        <div class="keys">
          <Key label="open the inbox" tone="primary" onclick={() => open("email", {})} />
          <Key label="open the jdex" onclick={() => open("vdex", {})} />
          <Key label="consult cfo" disabled title="the officer stage" />
        </div>
      {/if}
    </div>

    <div class="right">
      <div class="tiles">
        <Tile label="unread" value={inbox.length ? unreadRows.length : "—"} tone={unreadRows.length ? "primary" : inbox.length ? "contrast" : "support"} note={inbox.length ? `${unreadRows.filter((row) => row.trait.MESSAGE.channel === "email").length} mail · ${unreadRows.filter((row) => row.trait.MESSAGE.channel !== "email").length} chat` : "no mailbox"} />
        <Tile label="documents" value={documents.length} note="{coordinates} coordinates" />
        <Tile label="officers" value={officers.length} note={keyed ? `${officers.length} keyed · 0 bot` : `0 keyed · ${officers.length} bot`} />
      </div>
      {#if inbox.length}
        <div class="block">
          <Section label="inbox" count="{unreadRows.length} unread" />
          <div class="table">
            {#each inbox.slice(0, 3) as row (row.slug)}
              <button class="line" onclick={() => open(row.trait.MESSAGE.channel === "email" ? "email" : "chat", { open: row.slug, chat: row.trait.CHAT?.chat })}>
                <Pip size={6} tone={unread(row) ? "primary" : "muted"} glow={unread(row)} />
                <span class="glyph">{glyph(row)}</span>
                <span class="code">{who(row)}</span>
                <span class="sans">{subject(row)}</span>
                <span class="time">{when(row.trait.MESSAGE.at)}</span>
              </button>
            {/each}
          </div>
        </div>
      {:else}
        <Empty verb="no channels" trace="set VIVA_MAIL_IMAP · VIVA_MAIL_SMTP · SECRET_VIVA_TELEGRAM_TOKEN" dashed />
      {/if}
      {#if documents.length}
        <div class="block">
          <Section label="recent documents" />
          <div class="table">
            {#each documents.slice(0, 3) as row (row.slug)}
              <button class="line doc" onclick={() => open("vdex", { open: row.slug })}>
                <Pip size={4} tone={row.trait.DOCUMENT.format === "pdf" ? "warning" : "primary"} />
                <span class="code">{row.slug}</span>
                <span class="sans dim">{row.trait.DOCUMENT.title}</span>
                <span class="time">{age(row.trait.DOCUMENT.mtime)}</span>
              </button>
            {/each}
          </div>
        </div>
      {:else}
        <Empty verb="empty jdex" trace="drop the company's folders into the mountpoint · reboot" dashed />
      {/if}
      {#if officers.length}
        <div class="block">
          <Section label="on duty" />
          <div class="duty">
            {#each officers as held (held.slug)}
              <div class="duty-card"><Plate mark={held.slug.toUpperCase()} name={held.name.replace(/ Officer$/, "")} size="sm" muted={!keyed} state={{ label: keyed ? "keyed" : "bot", tone: keyed ? "primary" : "muted" }} /></div>
            {/each}
          </div>
        </div>
      {/if}
    </div>
  </div>
  <footer class="foot">
    <span class="tag">[VDEX]</span><span>{documents.length} documents · {coordinates} coordinates</span>
    <span class="rule"></span>
    <span class="tag">[MAIL]</span><span>{offices.email?.statics?.address ?? offices.email?.metadata?.address ?? "—"} · {inbox.filter((row) => row.trait.MESSAGE.channel === "email").length} messages</span>
    <span class="rule"></span>
    <span class="tag">[CHAT]</span><span>{(offices.chat?.statics?.platforms ?? ["—"]).join(" · ")}</span>
    <span class="spacer"></span>
    <span class="dim">daemon {daemon.slug} · {daemon.status?.code?.toLowerCase?.() ?? "—"}</span>
  </footer>
</div>

<script>
  import { Section, Key, Plate, Tile, Empty } from "@vivalence/drapes";

  let { terminal, daemon, mode, thread, buffer } = $props();

  let width = $state(0);
  const narrow = $derived(width > 0 && width < 820);

  let view = $state(buffer?.data?.view ?? "officers");
  let modes = $state([]);
  let counts = $state({});

  const keyed = (daemon.cortex?.find?.({ type: "verbatim" })?.length ?? 0) > 0;
  const LATER = [["CLO", "legal"], ["CSO", "strategy"], ["CAO", "administration · Körperschaft paperwork"], ["CPO", "product"], ["CMO", "marketing"], ["CIO", "information · the vdex steward"], ["CGO", "grants · 30-39_funding"]];
  const ONTOLOGY = { vdex: "vdex", email: "message · MAIL", chat: "message · CHAT", calendar: "event" };
  const TAG = { vdex: "[VDEX]", email: "[MAIL]", chat: "[CHAT]", calendar: "[CAL]" };

  const officers = $derived(modes.filter((held) => held.type === "officer"));
  const offices = $derived(modes.filter((held) => held.type === "office"));
  const chief = (held) => held.slug === "ceo";

  async function load() {
    modes = await daemon.entities.mode.find();
    const rows = await daemon.entities.literal.find({}, { limit: 5000 });
    const tally = {};
    for (const row of rows) {
      const key = row.ontology === "message" ? (row.trait.MESSAGE?.channel === "email" ? "email" : "chat") : row.ontology === "vdex" ? "vdex" : row.ontology === "event" ? "calendar" : row.ontology;
      const held = (tally[key] ??= { total: 0, unread: 0, coordinates: new Set(), upcoming: 0 });
      held.total += 1;
      if (row.symbol?.message?.status === "unread") held.unread += 1;
      if (row.symbol?.vdex?.id) held.coordinates.add(JSON.stringify(row.symbol.vdex.id));
      if (row.symbol?.event?.status === "upcoming") held.upcoming += 1;
    }
    counts = tally;
  }
  async function desk(held) {
    if (!held.call?.open) return;
    terminal.buffer = await held.call.open({ thread: thread?.id });
  }
  load();
</script>

<div class="voffice" class:narrow bind:clientWidth={width}>
  <header class="head">
    <div class="head-text">
      <h2>{view === "officers" ? "Officers" : "Offices"}</h2>
      <span class="sub">{view === "officers" ? "Chief officers operate the company. The chief consults; the rest answer /consult and never call out." : "One literal, three ontologies. Each office mode owns its rows, syncs them from the world on boot, and carries its own scheme symbols."}</span>
    </div>
    <div class="segment">
      <button class:on={view === "officers"} onclick={() => (view = "officers")}>officers</button>
      <button class:on={view === "offices"} onclick={() => (view = "offices")}>offices</button>
    </div>
    <span class="meta">{view === "officers" ? `${keyed ? officers.length : 0} keyed · ${keyed ? 0 : officers.length} bot` : `daemon ${daemon.slug} · @vcompany/domain/voffice`}</span>
  </header>

  {#if view === "officers"}
    <div class="grid three">
      {#each officers as held (held.slug)}
        <div class="card">
          <Plate mark={held.slug.toUpperCase()} name={held.name} description={held.description} size="lg" muted={!keyed} />
          <div class="traits">{#each held.traits ?? [] as trait}<span class="trait">{trait}</span>{/each}</div>
          <div class="kv">
            <span class="k">tool</span><span class="v primary">{chief(held) ? "— (the chief; arms the consults)" : `${held.slug}_consult {query, context}`}</span>
            <span class="k">type</span><span class="v">{held.type}/{held.slug}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="card-foot">
            <span class="state" class:primary={keyed}>{keyed ? "keyed" : "bot"}</span>
            <span class="spacer"></span>
            {#if chief(held)}<Key label="open desk" tone="primary" onclick={() => desk(held)} disabled={!held.call?.open} />{:else}<Key label="consult" tone="primary" disabled title="the officer stage" />{/if}
          </div>
        </div>
      {/each}
      {#if !officers.length}<Empty verb="no officers" trace="officer/{'{'}ceo,cfo,cto{'}'} — T0" />{/if}
    </div>
    <div class="later">
      <Section label="the officer stage · not landed" count={LATER.length} />
      <div class="grid seven">
        {#each LATER as [mark, description] (mark)}
          <div class="ghost"><span class="ghost-mark">{mark}</span><span class="ghost-desc">{description}</span></div>
        {/each}
      </div>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="grid five">
      {#each offices as held (held.slug)}
        {@const tally = counts[held.slug] ?? { total: 0, unread: 0, coordinates: new Set(), upcoming: 0 }}
        <div class="card">
          <div class="card-head"><span class="office-name">{held.name}</span><span class="office-slug">{held.type}/{held.slug}</span></div>
          <span class="desc">{held.description}</span>
          <div class="tiles">
            <Tile label={held.slug === "vdex" ? "documents" : held.slug === "calendar" ? "upcoming" : "unread"} value={held.slug === "vdex" ? tally.total : held.slug === "calendar" ? tally.upcoming : tally.unread} tone="primary" />
            <Tile label={held.slug === "vdex" ? "coordinates" : held.slug === "chat" ? "chats" : held.slug === "calendar" ? "events" : "messages"} value={held.slug === "vdex" ? tally.coordinates.size : tally.total} />
          </div>
          <div class="kv small">
            <span class="k">ontology</span><span class="v primary">{ONTOLOGY[held.slug] ?? "—"}</span>
            <span class="k">traits</span><span class="v">{(held.traits ?? []).length} · {(held.traits ?? []).slice(0, 3).join(" ")} …</span>
            <span class="k">mountpoint</span><span class="v cut">{held.mountpoint ?? held.metadata?.mountpoint ?? "minted"}</span>
            <span class="k">statics</span><span class="v cut">{Object.entries(held.statics ?? held.metadata?.statics ?? {}).map(([key, value]) => `${key} ${Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(" ") : value}`).join(" · ") || "—"}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="boot"><span class="tag">{TAG[held.slug] ?? `[${held.slug.toUpperCase()}]`}</span> {tally.total} rows{tally.unread ? ` · ${tally.unread} unread` : ""}</div>
        </div>
      {/each}
      <div class="card ghost-card">
        <div class="card-head"><span class="office-name">Ledger</span><span class="office-slug">office/transaction</span></div>
        <span class="desc">Bookkeeping. Ontology transaction, trait TRANSACTION, Σ entries = 0 in the repository. A fourth, empty slot — T3.</span>
        <div class="tiles"><Tile label="entries" value="—" tone="support" /><Tile label="accounts" value="—" /></div>
        <div class="boot"><span class="tag dim">[LEDGER]</span> not started · milestone slot</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>
